TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需先验全局参数知识、基于局部曲率最小和最大估计的轻量级自适应步长策略，适用于二次函数的梯度下降快速收敛，并证明其收敛到最优恒定步长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梯度下降在二次函数上的收敛速度高度依赖于步长的选择，而最优步长通常需要对函数全局参数（如Lipschitz常数和强凸常数）有先验知识。但实际中这些参数往往未知，因此需要一种不依赖先验知识的自适应步长策略，以实现快速收敛。

Method: 受平滑凸函数自适应步长算法启发，提出基于运行中的最小和最大局部曲率估计的轻量级计算策略。该策略在迭代过程中动态估计局部曲率，并据此调整步长，以逼近最优恒定步长。

Result: 证明了算法收敛到实现最快收敛速度的最优恒定步长。仿真结果表明，在二次函数情形下以及逻辑回归分类的初步测试中，该算法的收敛速度与近期自适应方法相当或更优。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于局部曲率估计的自适应步长策略能够在不依赖全局参数先验知识的情况下实现与最优恒定步长相当的收敛速度，且计算开销低，具有良好的实用性和竞争力。

Abstract: In this paper, we address the problem of achieving fast convergence in gradient descent for quadratic functions without relying on a priori knowledge of global function parameters. Inspired by adaptive stepsize algorithms for smooth convex functions, we propose a computationally lightweight strategy based on running estimates of minimal and maximal local curvatures. We prove that our proposed algorithm converges to the optimal constant stepsize which achieves the fastest convergence. Simulations show that the convergence rate achieved by our proposed algorithm is comparable or superior to recent adaptive approaches both in the quadratic case under consideration and in a preliminary test on logistic classification.

</details>


### [77] [Adaptive Stepsizes With Certified Convergence in Distributed Gradient Tracking With Quadratic Costs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03548)
*Yifan Wang,Luca Ballotta,Ruggero Carli,Andrea Iannelli,Xianghui Cao,Luca Schenato*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对标量二次问题上的分布式梯度跟踪算法，提出了一种具有收敛保证的自适应步长规则。该步长仅依赖于共识矩阵的本质谱半径和局部代价曲率的异质性（两者均可在线计算），在任意网络拓扑和曲率异质性下保证收敛，并在完全图与同质曲率情形下被证明是紧的。实验表明其显著优于现有离线与在线步长选择方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式梯度类算法的性能严重依赖步长选择，而现有理论界通常过于保守，实际实现往往依赖经验调参。虽然在线自适应策略已初步出现，但其性质与性能尚未被充分理解，缺乏可分析且具有收敛保证的自适应步长设计。

Method: 1) 聚焦于标量二次代价设定，显式刻画共识矩阵谱半径与曲率异质性之间的相互作用；2) 推导仅由本质谱半径和曲率异质性参数化的收敛界；3) 通过优化该收敛界获得收敛率与最优常数步长的计算上可处理的代理；4) 所得步长具有解析解释，并为完全图与同质曲率情形证明其紧性。

Result: 所提自适应步长在任意网络拓扑与曲率异质性下保证收敛，在完全图及同质曲率下证明是紧的（最优的）。大量数值仿真表明，该策略在所考虑设定下显著优于现有离线与在线步长选择规则。

Conclusion: 本文为分布式梯度跟踪提供了一种理论驱动、可在线计算且具有收敛保证的自适应步长方案，桥接了保守理论界与经验调参之间的鸿沟，并为更一般的分布式凸优化自适应步长设计提供了分析洞察。

Abstract: In this work, we propose an adaptive stepsize rule with guaranteed convergence for Distributed Gradient Tracking applied to scalar quadratic problems with heterogeneous curvatures. Most distributed gradient-based algorithms require a suitable stepsize selection. Available theoretical bounds are often overly conservative, while practical implementations typically rely on empirically tuned heuristics. Online adaptive strategies have only recently emerged for general distributed convex optimization, but their properties and performance remain only partially understood. To gain analytical insight, we focus on the informative setting of scalar quadratic costs, which allows us to explicitly capture the interplay between network topology and curvature heterogeneity. We derive a convergence bound parameterized only by the essential spectral radius of the consensus matrix and the heterogeneity of the local cost curvatures, both computable online without any prior knowledge of the optimization problem. Optimizing this bound yields a computationally tractable surrogate for the convergence rate and the optimal constant stepsize. The resulting stepsize admits an analytical interpretation, guarantees convergence for arbitrary network topologies and curvature heterogeneity, and is provably tight for complete graphs and homogeneous curvatures. Finally, extensive numerical simulations demonstrate that the proposed distributed adaptive strategy significantly outperforms existing offline and online stepsize selection rules in the considered setting.

</details>


### [78] [Sliding Methods for Hölder-Smooth Convex--Concave Minimax Optimization with Bilinear Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03846)
*Nhat Trung Nguyen,Alexander Gasnikov*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究具有双线性耦合的凸-凹极小极大优化问题，提出一种滑动（sliding）方法，利用问题的复合结构按各组件光滑性分别设定查询频率，在Hölder连续条件下给出收敛保证，并通过数值实验验证了复杂度边界与分离现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 凸-凹极小极大优化是博弈论、机器学习等领域的基础问题。实际中f和g可能具有不同的光滑性（从非光滑到光滑），现有方法通常以最差组件的复杂度为整体瓶颈，未能充分利用问题的复合结构。本文旨在设计一种能够根据各组件个体属性（Hölder指数、强凸性、耦合矩阵谱性质）分别分配查询频率的方法，以提升求解效率。

Method: 提出一种基于单调变分不等式递归滑动机制的滑移方法。该方法分别对f、g及双线性耦合算子对应的oracle以不同频率进行查询，频率由各组件的Hölder连续性、强凸性参数及耦合矩阵谱性质决定。分析覆盖非强凸与部分强凸情形；对随机问题，证明退化情形下的均匀期望间隙界，以及在环境光滑性和正有效曲率条件下收敛到显式噪声底。

Result: 建立了Hölder连续条件下方法的收敛保证，复杂度界显式依赖于Hölder指数、Hölder常数、强凸性参数及耦合矩阵谱性质。数值实验复现了理论预测的Hölder指数，确认各函数所需的梯度评估次数按其自身光滑度水平分离（而非取两者中较差者）。层析成像基准测试表明，当梯度评估比额外矩阵-向量乘积更昂贵时，方法可获得运行时增益。

Conclusion: 该方法成功实现了组件级复杂度分离，即在耦合结构中各组件的oracle查询复杂度由其自身光滑性决定，而非整体最差情形。这为双线性耦合的凸-凹极小极大问题提供了一种结构感知的高效求解框架，尤其在梯度评估昂贵、矩阵-向量乘积相对便宜的应用场景中优势明显。

Abstract: We study convex-concave minimax optimization problems with bilinear coupling of the form $\min_{x\in \mathcal X}\max_{y\in \mathcal Y} \; f(x)+\langle y,\mathbf{B}x\rangle-g(y),$ where the functions $f$ and $g$ have Hölder continuous (sub)gradients. This setting covers a broad range of regimes, from nonsmooth problems with bounded subgradient variation to smooth problems with Lipschitz continuous gradients; for a smooth component used in the coupling-induced regularizer, its Lipschitz-gradient constant is assumed to hold in the ambient space. We propose a sliding method that exploits the composite structure of the problem by querying the oracles associated with $f$, $g$, and the bilinear coupling operator at prescribed frequencies determined by their individual properties. The method is based on a recursive sliding scheme for monotone variational inequalities. We establish convergence guarantees under Hölder continuity and show how the resulting complexity bounds depend explicitly on the Hölder exponents, Hölder constants, strong convexity parameters, and spectral properties of the coupling matrix. Our analysis covers nonstrongly convex and partially strongly convex regimes. For stochastic problems, we prove a uniform expected-gap bound in the degenerate regime and, under ambient smoothness and positive effective curvature, convergence up to an explicit noise floor. Numerical experiments reproduce the predicted Hölder exponents and confirm that the number of gradient evaluations required for each function separates according to its own smoothness level rather than the worse of the two. A tomographic benchmark shows runtime gains when gradient evaluations are more expensive than the additional matrix-vector products.

</details>


### [79] [A Robust Chance Constrained Approach to Surgery Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03931)
*Marco Caserta,Antonio García Romero,Miguel Vaquero*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种稳健机会约束框架用于择期手术排程，将不确定性量化与排程优化分离：通过缓冲区引擎将分布信息转化为依赖可靠性的缓冲时长，排程模型同时决策分配、排序、开始时间及手术级可靠性级别。在Madrid HLA Moncloa医院的10个实例（45-227台手术）滚动滚动起始案例研究中，相比确定性均值基线，该框架将超过90分钟的延误降低97%，95分位延误减少约620分钟，总加班时间减少42%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于均值时长或固定缓冲规则的手术排程虽然在事前看起来高效，但在执行中容易变得脆弱——早期手术超时会在全天传播累积延误，影响后续手术。传统方法将可靠性作为固定服务级别参数，未能利用手术间的异质性差异来合理分配保护资源。

Method: 提出稳健机会约束框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 缓冲区引擎——将分布信息转化为依赖于可靠性水平的缓冲时长；(2) 排程模型——联合决策手术分配、排序、开始时间以及从离散菜单中选取各手术的可靠性水平。框架支持平均可靠性、最差日和硬目标三种风险姿态，使可靠性成为内生排程决策而非固定参数。

Result: 在西班牙Madrid HLA Moncloa医院的滚动起始案例研究中（10个实例，45至227台手术），与确定性均值基线和常见基准（统一可靠性和均匀比例缓冲）相比：超过90分钟的延误减少97%，95分位延误降低约620分钟，总加班时间减少42%。菜单式方法通过利用手术级异质性分配保护，避免不必要的保守性，从而产生价值。

Conclusion: 通过根据不确定性和运营暴露度分配缓冲区，该框架将异质手术时长数据和风险偏好转化为执行中更可靠且比一刀切规则更少保守缓冲的排程方案。可靠性作为内生决策因素，使得保护资源可以集中在运营价值最大的地方。

Abstract: We study elective surgery scheduling under uncertain procedure durations. Schedules based on mean durations or fixed buffering rules may appear efficient ex ante but become fragile in execution, as early overruns propagate through the day and expose later surgeries to accumulated delay. We propose a robust chance-constrained framework that separates uncertainty quantification from schedule optimization. A buffer engine converts distributional information into reliability-dependent buffered durations, while the scheduling model jointly selects assignments, sequences, start times, and surgery-level reliability levels from a discrete menu. Reliability therefore becomes an endogenous scheduling decision rather than a fixed service-level parameter. The framework accommodates average-reliability, worst-day, and hard-target risk postures. Comparisons with common-reliability and uniform proportional-buffer benchmarks show that the menu derives its value from exploiting surgery-level heterogeneity, allocating protection where it has the greatest operational value while avoiding unnecessary conservatism. In a rolling-origin case study at HLA Moncloa Hospital in Madrid, covering 10 instances with 45 to 227 surgeries, the approach reduces delays exceeding 90 minutes by 97%, lowers the 95th-percentile delay by approximately 620 minutes, and reduces total overtime by 42% relative to a deterministic mean-based baseline. By allocating buffers according to uncertainty and operational exposure, the framework translates heterogeneous duration data and risk preferences into schedules that are more reliable in execution and less conservatively buffered than one-size-fits-all rules.

</details>


### [80] [Simulation-Based Neural Policies for Portfolio Choice: Architecture, Training, and Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03933)
*Jules Viard,Alexander Michaelides,Panos Parpas*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了四种用于解决有限期随机控制问题（如生命周期消费储蓄和动态资产组合选择）的神经网络策略架构。不同于高维环境中无法验证的做法，作者在一个可精确求解的低维生命周期问题上进行对照实验，使用多种指标（福利、Bellman残差、形状约束和策略函数）综合评估各架构的优劣。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维经济决策问题（如生命周期消费储蓄、动态资产组合选择）受"维度灾难"困扰，传统动态规划网格方法在状态维度增高时计算成本呈指数增长。神经网络策略通过模拟直接优化被提出作为替代，但现有研究通常在高维环境下进行，由于没有参考解，无法隔离和诊断任何单一架构或训练选择的贡献。因此需要在可验证的低维环境中系统研究和比较不同架构与求解方法。

Method: 作者选取一个状态空间足够低维、能够通过动态规划精确求解的生命周期问题作为基准测试平台。比较四种神经网络架构：(1) 单一时间条件网络；(2) 在制度切换处拼接的两个网络；(3) 对每个日期分别训练一个网络，向冻结的下游策略反向学习；(4) 对逐日期架构施加约束的变体。训练采用方向主导的优化方法，将梯度幅值归一化。评估从四个维度进行：福利（实现效用目标）、无解参考的Bellman残差、形状限制（经济学约束）以及最终策略函数形态。

Result: 研究发现，不同架构即使实现相似的效用目标，也可能在是否尊重底层问题的经济学规律上存在显著差异。将策略按时间解耦（逐日期网络）使每个日期获得简短且适定的目标，配合方向主导优化效果良好。评估指标之间存在差异：仅看福利可能掩盖架构在经济学上是否合理的问题，因此需要联合采用福利、Bellman残差、形状约束和策略函数形态来全面评估架构设计。

Conclusion: 架构和求解方法的选择对经济决策问题的神经网络策略质量有重要影响，且需要在有参考解的低维环境中进行受控比较才能诊断各设计选择的贡献。逐时间解耦策略配合方向主导优化是一种有前景的架构设计范式。综合多维度评估（而非单纯依赖效用目标）对于确保神经网络策略在经济上合理至关重要。

Abstract: Many economic decision problems, lifecycle consumption-saving and dynamic portfolio choice, are finite-horizon stochastic control problems with continuous states and actions. When the state is low-dimensional these problems are solved by dynamic programming on a grid. The grid cost grows exponentially in the state dimension, known as the curse of dimensionality, which motivates replacing the value-function grid with a neural policy optimized directly through simulation. Such policies are usually studied in the high-dimensional settings that motivate them, precisely where no reference solution exists. So the contribution of any single architectural or training choice cannot be isolated and diagnosed. We therefore take a step back and treat both the architecture and the solution method as the objects of study. To this end, we consider a lifecycle problem with a sufficiently low-dimensional normalized state space to admit an accurate dynamic programming solution, which is used for evaluation. We compare four architectures. The simplest consists of a single time-conditioned network. We then consider two networks concatenated across the regime switch, followed by one network per date trained backward against frozen downstream policies. Finally, we evaluate a constrained variant of the per-date architecture. Decoupling the policy across time gives each date a short, well-posed objective, which we pair with direction-dominant optimization that normalizes away gradient magnitude. Architectures that lead to similar realized utility objective can nevertheless differ in whether they respect the underlying problem's economics. We therefore evaluate each design jointly based on welfare, a solution-free Bellman residual, shape restrictions, and the resulting policy functions.

</details>

### [81] [Light-Loco-Parkour: Versatile Perceptive Whole-Body Locomotion via Multi-Skill Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02653)
*Hongming Chen,Zhuoran Li,Hongxi Wang,Jiangpeng Hu,Ziliang Li,Peize Liu,QingRui Zhao,Xuhao Liu,Liang Pan,Ximin Lyu,Yuntao Ma,Tingxiang Fan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LLP是一个端到端感知全身运动系统，仅凭机载深度和速度命令，通过单个可部署策略实现人形机器人在杂乱地形上的行走、平衡、攀爬、下阶和跨越等全身运动技能，无需参考输入、技能标签或手工状态机，在仿真和真实世界中均表现出高成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人形全身控制系统在杂乱地形上的移动能力仍远不及人类：要么追踪表达丰富的全身参考轨迹但缺乏地形泛化能力，要么只能在线反应地形但手臂、躯干和膝盖几乎不被利用。作者希望弥合这一差距，实现无需参考输入即可自主决策并执行全身运动技能的单策略系统。

Method: LLP包含三项核心贡献：(1) 全身感知控制流水线，将RL训练的速度跟踪运动策略与从物体交互运动中学习的跑酷技能扩展，使同一策略能在开阔地形跟踪速度、在障碍物处执行全身穿越并在之后恢复行走；(2) 从稀疏种子运动出发，通过将单个运动扩展为跨越多种障碍几何形状的、动态可行的地形配对参考，而非依赖大规模运动语料库来获取地形条件技能；(3) 从奖励中自主学习技能转换，仅凭深度和命令决定何时及调用何种全身技能，无需one-hot技能标签、手工状态机或运行时运动生成器。

Result: 仿真和真实世界实验表明，该方法在基准地形和未见过的障碍物变体上均取得高成功率，且同一策略可零样本迁移到室内外硬件实验中，实现了仅凭机载感知和单个可部署策略在户外环境中的人形自主感知全身运动。

Conclusion: LLP首次实现了仅依靠机载感知和单一可部署策略的人形机器人在户外场景中的自主感知全身运动，验证了从稀疏种子扩展地形条件技能及从奖励学习自主技能转换的可行性，为下一代人形全身控制提供了新范式。

Abstract: Existing humanoid whole-body control systems still fall short of the way humans move through cluttered terrain: they either track expressive whole-body references without terrain generalization, or react to terrain online while leaving the arms, torso, and knees largely unused. We present \texttt{Light-Loco-Parkour} (LLP), an end-to-end perceptive whole-body locomotion system that closes this gap with a single deployable policy. Conditioned only on onboard depth and a velocity command, the policy decides when to walk, balance, climb, step down, or vault, with no reference input, skill label, hand-coded gate, or runtime motion graph. Compared with prior humanoid systems, LLP makes three contributions. First, it introduces a whole-body perceptive-control pipeline that extends an RL-trained, velocity-tracking locomotion policy with parkour skills learned from object-interacting motions, so the same policy tracks velocity in open terrain, executes whole-body traversal at obstacles, and resumes locomotion afterward. Second, it acquires terrain-conditioned skills from sparse seeds by expanding a single motion into dynamically feasible, terrain-paired references across obstacle geometry, rather than relying on a large motion corpus. Third, it learns autonomous skill transitions from reward, letting the policy decide when and which whole-body skill to invoke from depth and command alone, with no one-hot skill label, hand-coded state machine, or runtime motion generator. Simulation and real-world experiments show high success across both benchmarked terrains and unseen obstacle variations, and the same policy transfers zero-shot to indoor and outdoor hardware experiments. These results demonstrate autonomous perceptive whole-body locomotion on a humanoid in outdoor settings, using only onboard sensing and a single deployable policy.

</details>


### [82] [Semantic Haptic Feedback Enhances Dexterous Robotic Teleoperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02780)
*Bingjian Huang,Sahar Aseeri,Jonas Schmidtler,Joseph Zhang,Sonny Chan,Andrew Doxon,Jom Preechayasomboon,Evan Pezent,Alberto Rigo,Amir Memar,Nicholas Colonnese,Chase Tymms*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出"语义触觉"（semantic haptics）用于机器人遥操作，通过抽象触觉模式传达机器人状态的关键信息（分为"确认"和"异常"两类），利用气动和振动腕带实现触觉反馈。实验表明在单臂任务中性能与其他反馈方式相当，但在双臂任务中表现更优，可降低工作负荷、提升态势感知并获用户偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遥操作中的触觉反馈方法试图复刻真实交互中的高保真感官触觉，受限于传感和反馈硬件能力，且可能导致操作员工作负荷过高。作者希望突破这些局限，提出一种更简化的触觉反馈范式。

Method: 1) 提出"语义触觉"概念，用抽象触觉模式传达机器人状态关键信息；2) 将机器人状态归类为"确认"（Confirmation）和"异常"（Exception）两类；3) 在机器人仿真中实现模块化触觉渲染管线；4) 通过气动和振动腕带向操作员传递语义触觉反馈；5) 进行三项评估研究，识别最有效的语义触觉设计，并与其他反馈方式（感官触觉、视觉反馈）进行对比。

Result: 在常见的抓取-放置遥操作任务中，语义触觉在单臂任务中与其他反馈方式性能相当；在双臂任务中性能优于其他方式，具体表现为降低任务工作负荷、提升态势感知，并获得操作员的整体偏好。

Conclusion: 语义触觉作为一种简化硬件需求、支持触觉模式与机器人状态一对多映射的反馈方法，在遥操作尤其是双臂任务中具有显著优势，为触觉反馈设计提供了新的研究方向。

Abstract: In robot teleoperation, haptic feedback can be used to help human operators accomplish dexterous manipulation tasks. However, existing haptic feedback methods try to replicate high-fidelity sensory haptics that are felt in real world interactions, which are constrained by the sensing and feedback hardware capability and may lead to higher workload.
  To addresses these limitations, this work introduces semantic haptics for teleoperation, which uses abstract haptic patterns to convey critical information about robot states. We categorize robot states into "Confirmations" and "Exceptions", implement a modular haptic rendering pipeline in robot simulation, and deliver semantic haptic feedback to operators through pneumatic and vibrotactile wristbands. This simplifies hardware requirements and enables one-to-many mappings between haptic patterns and robot states.
  Through three evaluation studies, we identify the most effective semantic haptic design for a common pick and place teleoperation task and compare semantic haptics to other teleoperation feedback approaches including sensory haptics and visual feedback. Results suggest that while semantic haptics performs similarly as other feedback in unimanual tasks, it achieves superior performance in bimanual tasks, with reduced task workload, increased situational awareness, and overall preference.

</details>


### [83] [Toward Certified Functional Safety for Industrial Humanoid Robots: The Fail-Passive Gap and a Feasibility Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02809)
*Caiwu Ding,Tao Cui,Lingyun Wang,Chengtao Wen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文指出工业人形机器人功能安全认证的核心障碍是"fail-passive gap"——腿足机器人的安全状态是主动受控状态，违反ISO 13849-1/EN 60204-1中失电即安全的被动失效假设。作者用经过认证的外部安全链（光幕、急停、失效安全PLC等）作为工具精确定位这一问题，并在Unitree G1 EDU拾放单元上验证，最终将不可认证的残差定位到SDA与平衡策略之间的接口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业人形机器人的瓶颈不在运动或操作能力，而在腿足平台功能安全认证的不成熟。根本困难在于腿足机器人的安全状态是主动受控状态，断电会导致失控摔倒，本身即是危险，违背了ISO 13849-1/EN 60204-1所依赖的fail-passive假设。

Method: 作者使用一个经认证的外部安全链（光 curtain、急停、fail-safe输入、fail-safe PLC、无线PROFIsafe）作为定位工具，因为该外部链是封闭且可用既有方法（PFHD、DC、CCF、PL/SILCL）量化的，从而将不可认证的残余元素精确定位到机器人侧反应链。通过西门子fail-safe S7-1500急停参考实现，展示其可认证的Reaction子系统是基于接触器的断电（Stop Category 0）——恰是平衡人形机器人无法具备的元素。在Unitree G1 EDU上部署工业软件定义自动化（SDA）控制器并与平衡策略共置。

Result: 在3m x 1.5m半封闭工作空间的Unitree G1 EDU拾放单元上验证了该方法。贡献了人形特异的主动安全状态分析（摔倒即危险、单支撑停止边界、平衡策略残余风险、ISO 13855安全间距）以及带来源标注的时序预算。发现由于G1机载计算非安全级硬件，PROFIsafe接收端点不是认证的安全运行时，这反而强化而非解决了fail-passive gap，并将其定位于SDA到平衡策略的接口。作者明确不声称端到端认证PL e/SIL 3。

Conclusion: 该工作的价值在于精确划定fail-passive gap的位置——它位于外部认证安全链与机器人侧反应链之间，并进一步收缩到SDA与平衡策略的接口。通过明确承认当前无法实现端到端功能安全认证，为后续研究指明了攻克主动安全状态形式化认证这一核心难题的方向。

Abstract: Industrial humanoid robots are constrained less by locomotion or manipulation capability than by the immaturity of functional safety certification for legged platforms. The root difficulty is that the safe state of a legged robot is an actively-controlled state, which violates the fail-passive assumption underlying ISO~13849-1 / EN~60204-1: removing power from a walking biped causes an uncontrolled fall, so classical de-energization is itself a hazard. We term this the fail-passive gap and use a certified external safety chain (light curtain, emergency stop, fail-safe input, fail-safe PLC, and wireless PROFIsafe) as an instrument to locate it precisely: because the external chain is closed and quantifiable with established methods (PFHD, DC, CCF, PL/SILCL), the residual uncertifiable element is pinpointed to the robot-side reaction chain. Using a Siemens fail-safe S7-1500 emergency-stop reference, we show its certifiable Reaction subsystem is contactor-based power removal (Stop Category~0)---exactly the element a balancing humanoid cannot have. We deliberately do not claim end-to-end certified PL~e / SIL~3. We validate the approach on a Unitree G1 EDU pick-and-place cell in a 3m x 1.5m semi-enclosed workspace, and contribute a humanoid-specific analysis of the active safe state (fall-as-hazard, single-support stop bounds, balancing-policy residual risk, ISO~13855 separation) and a provenance-labeled timing budget. Hosting an industrial software-defined automation (SDA) controller on the robot, co-located with the balancing policy, moves robot-side PROFINET/PROFIsafe reception onto a standardized IEC~61131-3 interface; because the G1's onboard compute is not safety-rated hardware, this endpoint is not a certified safety runtime, which reinforces rather than resolves the fail-passive gap and localizes it to the SDA-to-balancing-policy interface.

</details>


### [84] [Staying on Spec: Real-Time Monitoring under Uncertainty with a Maritime Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02811)
*Elizabeth Dietrich,Hanna Krasowski,Emir Cem Gezer,Roger Skjetne,Asgeir Johan Sørensen,Murat Arcak*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于数据驱动的可达集（reachable set）的实时监控框架，用于在不确定性下评估复杂任务与安全规范，并在海事导航场景中实例化，通过仿真与硬件实验验证了相比现有方法的更优风险检测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人系统必须在不确定性下运行同时满足复杂任务和安全规范。现有的规范监控方法通常需要大量数据或明确的概率分布，这在现实部署中难以满足，因此需要一种在数据受限条件下仍能有效监控规范的方法。

Method: 提出一个实时监控框架，核心思想是利用数据驱动的可达集来代替显式不确定性分布，从而降低数据需求。框架在海事导航中实例化，处理源于交通规则的复杂规范。具体包括：一个数据高效的到达集构建管道，以及一个适合实时部署的监控公式。

Result: 仿真与硬件实验表明，该框架在现实扰动下能够实现鲁棒监控，与最先进的方法相比，风险检测能力得到提升。

Conclusion: 数据驱动的可达集可以有效支撑实时规范监控，在数据需求与传统方法的强假设之间提供了可行的折中，并在海事导航这一实际场景中得到验证。

Abstract: Robotic systems must operate under uncertainty while satisfying complex task and safety specifications. Monitoring such specifications under uncertainty remains challenging, as existing formulations typically require extensive data or explicit uncertainty distributions. In this paper, we propose a real-time monitoring framework that reduces data requirements by leveraging data-driven reachable sets for specification evaluation. We instantiate the framework for maritime navigation, where complex specifications arise from traffic rules. We develop a data-efficient pipeline for constructing reachable sets and derive a monitoring formulation suitable for real-time deployment. Simulation and hardware experiments demonstrate robust monitoring under realistic disturbances, achieving improved risk detection compared to state-of-the-art metrics.

</details>


### [85] [Biconvex Optimization for Smooth Minimum-Time Trajectories around Convex Obstacles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02834)
*Peter Werner,Tobia Marcucci,Daniela Rus*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种双凸（biconvex）方法用于凸障碍物周围的最小时间运动规划。该方法通过变量变换将最小时间目标和所有导数约束联合凸化，并利用时变分离平面处理碰撞避免，将问题简化为双凸规划。通过交替计算最大间隔分离平面和优化轨迹来求解，且仅添加当前迭代碰撞的障碍物的平面，使轨迹可以绕过障碍物并逃离局部极小值。该方法从简单无碰撞多边形曲线出发保证收敛，在无人机导航和双臂卸载实验中计算时间与最先进的分解式运动规划器相当，但处理问题类别更广且对不良初始化更鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的运动规划方法在处理最小时间规划时面临非凸优化难题，特别是在障碍物环境中保证收敛性和鲁棒性方面存在挑战。作者旨在提出一种保证收敛、支持任意高阶导数约束且对初始化鲁棒的最小时间运动规划方法，同时保持与现有分解式规划器相当的计算效率。

Method: 提出双凸方法：1）通过变量变换联合凸化最小时间目标和所有阶导数约束；2）利用时变分离平面处理碰撞避免，将问题转化为双凸规划；3）交替求解：计算最大间隔分离平面 + 优化轨迹；4）仅添加当前迭代碰撞到的障碍物的平面，使轨迹能够跳跃绕障并逃离局部极小值。

Result: 在无人机导航和双臂卸箱实验中，该方法可靠地产生高质量轨迹，计算时间与最先进的基于分解的运动规划器相当，同时处理更大类别的问题，并且对不良初始化具有更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该方法从简单的无碰撞多边形曲线出发保证收敛，兼顾了收敛性保证、实时计算效率和鲁棒性，为最小时间运动规划提供了一种新的有效方案。

Abstract: We present a biconvex approach for minimum-time motion planning around convex obstacles that is guaranteed to converge, is anytime, and supports derivative constraints to arbitrary order. We jointly convexify the minimum-time objective and all derivative constraints through a change of variables, and handle collision avoidance via time-varying separating planes, reducing the problem to a biconvex program. This program is solved by alternating between computing maximum-margin separating planes and optimizing the trajectory. By only adding planes for obstacles that the current iterate collides with, the trajectory can jump around obstacles and escape local minima. The method is guaranteed to converge starting from a simple collision-free polygonal curve. In our experiments on drone navigation and dual-arm bin unloading, we find that the proposed method reliably produces high-quality trajectories with computation times comparable to state-of-the-art decomposition-based motion planners, while handling a larger class of problems and being substantially more robust to bad initialization. Project page:https://wernerpe.github.io/bmtp-website/

</details>


### [86] [Control Barrier Functions via Minkowski Operations for Safe Navigation among Polytopes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02886)
*Yi-Hsuan Chen,Shuo Liu,Wei Xiao,Calin Belta,Michael Otte*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对多面体（polytopic）机器人和多面体障碍物的精确有符号距离函数（SDF）公式，并将其与非光滑控制屏障函数（CBF）集成，用于机器人安全导航。通过Minkowski运算和伴随凸规划计算精确SDF，并利用灵敏度分析推导出统一的解析梯度表达式，从而实现了非保守的安全控制和安全性恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的CBF方法通常用保守的平滑形状（如球体或椭圆体）近似多面体以获得显式可微距离函数，这种做法过于保守。在保证系统动力学、控制和精确几何的前提下安全导航多面体环境仍是一个挑战，需要直接的精确SDF公式来处理多边形机器人-多边形障碍物的碰撞规避问题。

Method: 1) 利用Minkowski运算，在无碰撞（正号）和碰撞（负号）两种情况下，通过伴随凸规划计算精确SDF。2) 利用2D Minkowski运算的几何性质和两个伴随凸规划的最优性条件，通过灵敏度分析推导出精确SDF梯度的统一解析表达式。3) 将精确SDF与非光滑CBF集成，构建安全控制策略。4) 通过纯平移和三种独轮车模型场景验证有效性。

Result: 在纯平移场景和三种独轮车模型场景（不安全初始化的恢复、单障碍物和多障碍物规避）中验证了有效性。与基线方法对比显示，所提框架能够实现非保守的机动和安全性恢复。此外，精确旋转梯度的推导揭示了由几何与非完整运动学耦合引起的、此前被掩盖的一类局部极小值。

Conclusion: 本文提出的精确SDF框架克服了传统CBF方法中使用保守近似形状的限制，通过精确的几何表示和非光滑CBF相结合，实现了更高效的安全导航，并揭示了新的理论见解（几何与非完整运动学耦合导致的局部极值问题）。

Abstract: Safely navigating polytopic environments while respecting the dynamics, control, and exact geometry of the underlying system is a challenge in robotics. Control barrier functions (CBFs) synthesize safe control policies by rendering the safe set forward invariant, but many existing CBF-based methods approximate polytopes using conservative smooth shapes, such as spheres or ellipsoids, to obtain explicit differentiable distance functions. In this article, we propose an exact Signed Distance Function (SDF) formulation for a {\it polytopic} robot and {\it polytopic} obstacles and integrate it with nonsmooth CBFs. Leveraging Minkowski operations, the proposed method computes the exact SDF via companion convex programs in both the collision-free (positive-sign) and in-collision (negative-sign) cases. Furthermore, by exploiting the convenient geometric properties of 2D Minkowski operations and the optimality conditions of the two companion convex programs, we derive a unified analytical expression for the gradient of the exact SDF via sensitivity analysis. The exact rotational gradient further reveals a previously masked class of local minima induced by the coupling between geometry and nonholonomic kinematics. We demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework through a pure-translation case and three scenarios with unicycle models involving recovery from an unsafe initialization and single- and multiple-obstacle avoidance. Comparisons with baseline methods highlight how the proposed framework enables non-conservative maneuvers and safety recovery.

</details>


### [87] [Contact-Driven Localization in a Freeform Robotic Self-Assembled Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02895)
*Mohammadali Rashidioun,Michael Sosa,Petras Swissler*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种仅依赖二元接触信息（机器人是否物理连接）的模块化机器人接触驱动定位方法，通过虚拟力框架使机器人迭代优化位姿——对已连接邻居施加吸引力、对未连接邻居施加排斥力，实现无需外部基础设施和高端传感器的可扩展自由形态自组装定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 群机器人尤其是自重构系统中的精确相对定位仍然困难，现有方法依赖外部追踪基础设施或高成本传感器，限制了可扩展性和在非结构化环境中的部署。

Method: 利用局部通信中的二元接触信息（两机器人是否物理连接）作为定位线索，构建虚拟力框架：机器人向已对接邻居吸引、向未对接邻居排斥，在迭代中逐步精炼自身位姿。

Result: 仿真结果表明该方法在塔和悬臂结构组装过程中能有效实现定位，支持精确、可扩展、自由形式的自组装。

Conclusion: 该接触驱动定位方法无需外部基础设施、仅依赖最小化的机载感知即可实现有效的模块化机器人自组装定位，为无外部设施环境下的大规模自由形态自重构提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Accurate localization remains a key challenge in swarm robotics, particularly for self-reconfigurable systems that must identify relative positions to form diverse structures. Most existing approaches rely on external tracking infrastructure or high-cost sensors, which limit scalability and deployment in unstructured environments. In this paper, we propose a novel contact-driven localization method for modular robots that leverages only local communication through binary contact information (whether two robots are physically connected or not). To exploit these contact cues, we introduce a virtual-force framework in which robots iteratively refine their poses attracting toward dock-connected neighbors and repelling from non-connected ones. The method requires no external infrastructure and relies only on minimal onboard sensing. Simulations show effective localization during the assembly of towers and cantilevers, enabling accurate, scalable, free-form self-assembly.

</details>


### [88] [DeRP: An Algorithm for Self-Assembly of Power-Delivery Networks using Recursive Branching in Information-Limited Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02904)
*Mohammadali Rashidioun,Sangwoo Park,Petras Swissler*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Dendritic Recursive Pivoting (DeRP)，一种基于局部通信和方位感知的分散式框架，用于机器人群体自主组装多目标电力输送网络。网络从公共电源出发，在局部选择的枢轴点（近似Steiner点）处分叉并递归扩展，无需全局规划。实验表明其网络总长度渐近约为全局最小值的125%，功率损耗降至欧氏Steiner树的65%，且在100个Sink以内扩展呈亚线性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统有线网络预规划和电池方案在非结构化野外环境中为分布式设备持续供电面临基础设施和后勤保障的重大挑战，需要一种不依赖全局规划和完整知识的自适应电力输送方案。

Method: DeRP采用树状递归枢轴（Dendritic Recursive Pivoting）策略：机器人作为电力导管，仅依靠局部通信和朝向Sink的方位感知，在局部选定的枢轴点处分叉形成分支，这些分支近似Steiner树的Steiner点，从而高效路由至多个Sink。分叉操作递归执行，实现无需全局规划的可扩展自适应网络形成。

Result: DeRP形成的网络长度渐近约为全局最小解的125%，功率损耗相对于欧氏Steiner树降低至65%；在多达100个Sink与机器人数量增长的场景下，模拟完成时间呈亚线性扩展。

Conclusion: DeRP能够在传统基础设施部署困难的场景中实现弹性、自适应的电力输送，为野外环境下的分布式供电提供了一种可行的分散式替代方案。

Abstract: Delivering sustained power to distributed equipment in unstructured field environments using pre-planned wired networks or battery-based solutions presents significant infrastructure and logistics challenges. This paper presents Dendritic Recursive Pivoting (DeRP), a decentralized framework for multi-target network formation in robot swarms based solely on local communication and bearing-based sensing toward sinks. We envision a system in which robots, acting as a conduit, self-assemble a power network from a common source, forming branches at locally selected pivot points that approximate the Steiner points of Steiner trees to efficiently route to multiple Sinks. This branching operation is performed recursively to enable scalable and adaptive network formation without global planning. The proposed method is evaluated in terms of the total network length and estimated power loss, and is quantitatively compared against global baselines such as the Minimum Spanning Tree and Steiner tree solutions (GeoSteiner), which require complete knowledge of Sink locations. Specifically, we found that the networks formed by DeRP asymptotically form approximately 125\% of the global minimum length while reducing power losses to 65\% relative to Euclidean Steiner trees. In addition, we empirically characterize scaling behavior by measuring simulation completion time as the number of Sinks and robots increases, and find that this scaling was sub-linear for up to 100 sinks. The proposed approach enables resilient, adaptive power delivery in environments where deployment of traditional infrastructure is challenging.

</details>


### [89] [ValueFormer: A Causal Transformer Value Function with Stage-Aware Labels for Semi-Autonomous Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02958)
*Inkyu Sa,Konstantin Stulov,Rajat Bhageria*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ValueFormer，一个策略无关的紧凑因果Transformer，在冻结的DINOv3骨干上每个前向传播同时输出两个逐帧信号：用于优势估计的平滑蒙特卡洛价值V_mc和用于在线错误检测的尖锐二值信号。通过阶段感知的成功-后衰减回报标注失败episode，并在真实机器人双臂三明治组装任务上将任务完成率从70%提升至85%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA策略通过行为克隆训练会出现静默失败——仅从动作流中无法区分崩溃回滚与正常进展，因为模仿学习缺乏"进度"的概念。强化学习虽然能提供进度信号，但在真实机器人场景中经验成本高昂且形变食品难以仿真。终端成败位虽然可学习但过于稀疏，无法指出rollout错误发生的时间点。作者认为逐帧标签而非网络架构才是关键难点。

Method: 1) 提出ValueFormer：紧凑的、策略无关的因果Transformer，基于冻结的DINOv3骨干，单次前向同时输出两类逐帧信号——平滑的MC价值V_mc用于优势估计，以及尖锐的二值信号用于在线错误检测，两类目标在设计中刻意相互对立。2) 标注策略：失败episode采用阶段感知的成功-后衰减回报，保留失败阶段之前的成功曲线；检测通过失误区间而非单一失败时间点进行监督，使策略从中恢复的失误也能携带训练信号。3) 推理优化：批处理bf16编码器将在线服务成本降低3-5倍。

Result: 在真实机器人双臂三明治组装任务（1,427个episode）上，由critic推导的逐帧训练权重将任务完成率从70%提升至85%（n=20时在噪声范围内）；批处理bf16编码器将在线服务成本削减3-5倍，使critic能在单GPU上与策略一起以2Hz运行。

Conclusion: 逐帧信号的密度、连续性与形状设计是提升VLA行为克隆策略的关键，ValueFormer证明了紧凑的critic模型无需强化学习即可为模仿策略提供有效的进度信号与错误检测能力，并能在实际部署中与策略并行实时运行。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies trained by behavior cloning fail silently: from the action stream alone, a collapsing rollout looks much like one making clean progress, because imitation supplies no notion of progress. Reinforcement learning would supply one, but it is impractical here, where real-robot experience is costly and deformable food resists simulation. The cheap alternative, a terminal success / failure bit, is learnable in principle yet far too sparse to say when a rollout went wrong. We argue that the per-frame label, not the architecture, is the hard part: to be useful it must be dense, continuous, and correctly shaped. We present ValueFormer, a compact policy-agnostic causal transformer over a frozen DINOv3 backbone that emits two per-frame signals in one forward pass: a smooth Monte Carlo value, V_mc, for advantage estimation and a sharp binary value for online mistake detection, targets that pull in opposite directions by design. Failed episodes are labeled with a stage-aware, success-then-decay return that preserves the success curve before the failure stage, and detection is supervised from mistake intervals rather than a single failure time, so mistakes the policy recovers from also carry signal. On a real-robot bimanual sandwich-assembly task 1,427 episodes), a critic-derived per-frame training weight lifts task completion from 70% to 85% (within noise at n=20), and a batched bf16 encoder cuts the live serving cost 3~5 times so the critic runs at 2 Hz alongside the policy on a single GPU.

</details>


### [90] [EmbodiedVAE: Disentangled Video VAE for Efficient and Controllable Embodied Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02990)
*Jiayi Luo,Hanxin Zhu,Chen Gao,Jiankun Wang,Cong Wang,Tianyu He,Jianxin Li,Zhibo Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EmbodiedVAE是一种针对机器人操作世界模型设计的视频VAE，采用双编码器-单解码器架构和非对称时空压缩模块，将机器人手臂运动与背景环境解耦，实现紧凑且可控的潜在表示，并引入基于最优传输的一致性模块保持运动时序一致性，在重建质量和动作控制精度上超越现有视频VAE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜扩散模型（LDMs）依赖为自然场景优化的VAE，无法适应具身操作场景的独特特征，导致潜在表示既不紧凑也不可控，阻碍了LDM的高效训练和精确的机器人控制。

Method: EmbodiedVAE采用双编码器-单解码器架构，配备非对称时空压缩模块自动解耦机器人手臂运动与背景环境，实现整体紧凑性的同时提供显式具身潜在表示以支持细粒度动作控制；另外引入基于最优传输的一致性模块，显式强制运动保真度和帧间一致性。

Result: 在机器人操作场景中，EmbodiedVAE相比最先进的视频VAE平均获得2dB的PSNR提升，实现了更高压缩率下的卓越重建质量和更精确的动作控制。

Conclusion: 通过解耦运动与背景并强化时序一致性，EmbodiedVAE为机器人操作世界模型提供了紧凑且可控的潜在表示，验证了为具身操作场景专门设计视频VAE的有效性。

Abstract: Latent diffusion models (LDMs) have recently significantly advanced embodied learning in constructing powerful embodied manipulation world models. However, despite the remarkable performance, existing LDMs predominantly rely on Variational Autoencoders (VAEs) optimized for natural scenes while failing to account for the unique characteristics of embodied manipulation scenarios, yielding latent representations that are neither compact nor controllable, thereby hindering efficient training of LDMs and precise robotic control. To solve this problem, we present EmbodiedVAE, a novel video VAE that provides compact yet controllable latent representations tailored for the robotic manipulation world models. Specifically, EmbodiedVAE adopts a dual-encoder, single-decoder architecture with an asymmetric spatio-temporal compression module, which automatically disentangles the robot arm's motion from background environment, resulting in overall compactness while providing explicit embodied latent to support fine-grained action control. To further preserve the temporal consistency of learned robotic motion latent, we introduce an optimal-transport-based consistency module that explicitly enforces motion fidelity and inter-frame coherence. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our proposed EmbodiedVAE achieves superior reconstruction quality with high compression rate, while enabling more precise action control in robotic manipulation scenarios with an average of 2dB PSNR improvement over state-of-the-art video VAEs.

</details>


### [91] [A Wearable Stiffness-Rendering Haptic Device with a Honeycomb Jamming Mechanism for Bilateral Teleoperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03002)
*Thomas M. Kwok,Bohan Zhang,Wai Tuck Chow*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于蜂窝卡纸机制（honeycomb jamming）的轻量化可穿戴触觉设备HJ-Haptic（仅20g），通过30 kPa真空压力实现1.15–2.64 N/mm的刚度调节，用于双边遥操作中的运动觉（kinesthetic）反馈，使操作员能够实时感知物体刚度并调整抓握力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双边遥操作中运动觉反馈的缺失是操作员难以准确感知远端物体刚度、无法自然调节抓握力的核心挑战。现有触觉设备往往笨重、功耗高或安全性不足，亟需一种轻量、安全且能实时渲染刚度的可穿戴解决方案。

Method: 采用蜂窝卡纸机构实现刚度可变；通过30 kPa真空压力使设备刚度在1.15–2.64 N/mm范围内连续变化；将设备集成到遥操作框架中，利用三点点弯曲试验验证蜂窝卡纸机构的力学性能，并通过遥操作物体抓取任务评估设备功能。

Result: 实验结果显示遥操作运动、刚度渲染和交互力反馈具有较小的均方根误差（RMSE）和强相关性；HJ-Haptic能够根据实时夹具反馈调整自身刚度，成功模拟直接用手抓取物体的感觉。

Conclusion: HJ-Haptic验证了蜂窝卡纸机制在触觉反馈中的潜力，真空压力驱动的设计确保了操作安全性（气体泄漏或材料失效时不会造成危险），该方案不仅适用于遥操作场景，还可扩展到扩展现实（XR）环境中的交互应用。

Abstract: This paper addresses the challenge of providing kinesthetic feedback in bilateral teleoperation by designing a wearable, lightweight (20 g), and compact haptic device, the HJ-Haptic, utilizing a honeycomb jamming mechanism for object stiffness rendering. The HJ-Haptic device can vary its stiffness, from 1.15 N/mm to 2.64 N/mm, using a 30 kPa vacuum pressure. We demonstrate its implementation in a teleoperation framework, enabling operators to adjust grip force based on a reliable haptic feedback on object stiffness. A three-point flexural test on the honeycomb jamming mechanism and teleoperated object-grasping tasks were conducted to evaluate the device's functionality. Our experiments demonstrated a small RMSE and strong correlations in teleoperated motion, stiffness rendering, and interaction force feedback. The HJ-Haptic effectively adjusts its stiffness in response to real-time gripper feedback, mimicking the sensation of direct object grasping with hands. The device's use of vacuum pressure ensures operator safety by preventing dangerous outcomes in case of gas leakage or material failure. Incorporating the HJ-Haptic into the teleoperation framework provided the reliable perception of object stiffness and stable teleoperation. This study highlights the potential of the honeycomb jamming mechanism for enhancing haptic feedback in various applications, including teleoperation scenarios, as well as interactions with extended-reality environments.

</details>


### [92] [Forbidden Region Dynamic Active Constraints in Robot-Assisted Minimally Invasive Surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03010)
*Zejian Cui,Ferdinando Rodriguez y Baena*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种能量耗散的禁忌区域主动约束（FRAC）管线设计，利用深度感应相机适应呼吸运动引起的动态组织变形，通过细网格表示（12万多个多边形）在43.48Hz下运行，并在体外轨迹跟踪实验中验证了其任务安全性和相比传统简单几何FRAC方法的鲁棒性与时间效率优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的FRAC策略在机器人辅助手术中面临动态组织变形、能量无源性保持、实现速度等挑战，限制了其在临床遥操作手术中的应用。需要一种能够适应呼吸等生理运动引起的组织变形的FRAC方案，同时保证能量耗散特性和实时性。

Method: 提出了一个能量耗散的FRAC管线，利用深度感应相机实时捕捉动态组织变形，采用包含122,806个多边形的细网格表示来精确描述禁忌区域，并以43.48Hz的频率运行。设计了由"虚拟"外科医生执行的体外轨迹跟踪实验进行定量评估，并与基于简单几何AC表示的FRAC方法进行对比研究。

Result: 在所有试验中成功维持了预设的安全距离，验证了任务安全性。对比研究表明该方法在所有试验中都能提供更稳健、有效的引导，同时时间成本与简单几何FRAC方法相当甚至更低。

Conclusion: 所提出的能量耗散FRAC策略能够适应动态组织变形，在保证任务安全性的前提下提供更鲁棒高效的引导，且具有与简单方法可比的时间效率，为临床遥操作手术中的FRAC应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: In robot-assisted surgery, Forbidden Region Active Constraints (FRAC) represent a control strategy that helps maintain task safety by generating anisotropic haptic guidance to surgeons. However, several challenges need to be overcome before FRAC can benefit teleoperative surgery in a clinical setting. These challenges include the ability to allow for dynamic tissue deformation, maintain energetic passivity, and speed of implementation, among others. In this study, we propose the pipeline design for an energy dissipative FRAC strategy, which accommodates the dynamic tissue deformation caused by respiratory movements, by utilizing a depth sensing camera. The proposed FRAC strategy adopts a fine mesh representation, with a total number of 122,806 polygons in the case study presented, while running at 43.48Hz. We designed in vitro trajectory tracking experiments conducted by a "virtual" surgeon to aid quantitative assessment of the method, including its effectiveness in maintaining task safety, which was confirmed by successfully maintaining a pre-defined safety distance across all trials. We also conducted comparative studies to investigate the robustness and time-efficiency of our method against other FRAC methods that rely on simple geometry AC representations. We demonstrate that our method provides a more robust and effective guidance overall, while maintaining comparable, if not lower, time costs.

</details>


### [93] [PACE: Adaptive Budget Allocation for Time-Efficient Embodied Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03034)
*Yuchen Huang,Xijiang Ying,Zhenhua Ma,Xiaxiang Yuan,Zhijie Gao,Jiayi Huang,Ruichi Mao,Jiazheng Zhang,Hongsheng Ti,Maotao Tian,Rong Shi,Lu Zhao,Shizhuang Zhang,Zhuo Cui,He Wang,Ling Liu,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出PACE框架，通过交错推理-执行架构和动态预算分配器，将推理时间隐藏在执行窗口中，在保持规划质量的同时显著提升推理速度（6.9倍加速），在Robotouille基准上成功率达10%，较基线提升67%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理增强的大语言模型在规划任务上表现出色，但在具身系统部署中因推理延迟过长（常超过每分钟）而不切实际。根本瓶颈在于现有范式的串行特性——模型必须完成所有推理后才能执行，执行时间窗口完全未被利用。

Method: 提出PACE框架，包含两个关键创新：(1) Interleaved Think-Act架构，将认知处理与动作执行流水线化；(2) Dynamic Budget Allocator，根据可用执行时间窗口动态调整推理token预算。

Result: 在Robotouille基准上使用Qwen3-8B-AWQ，PACE取得10%成功率（比ReAct+Think基线提升67%），推理时间加速6.9倍，66.8%的思考时间被隐藏在执行窗口内。

Conclusion: 时间感知的架构创新能够使推理模型在延迟敏感的具身领域发挥作用，战略性地分配认知努力可以同时提升规划质量和时间效率。

Abstract: Reasoning-enhanced large language models have achieved remarkable improvements in planning tasks, yet their deployment in embodied systems remains impractical due to prohibitive inference delays-often exceeding minutes per planning instance. The fundamental bottleneck stems from the serial nature of existing paradigms: models must complete all reasoning before any action execution, leaving execution time windows entirely unexploited. We introduce PACE (Planning with Adaptive Cognitive Effort), a framework that enables interleaved reasoning and execution through two key innovations: an Interleaved Think-Act architecture that pipelines cognitive processing with action execution, and a Dynamic Budget Allocator that adapts reasoning token budgets to available execution time windows. On the Robotouille benchmark using Qwen3-8B-AWQ, PACE achieves a 10% success rate-representing a 67% improvement over the ReAct+Think baseline-while delivering 6.9 times acceleration in thinking time compared to unconstrained reasoning. The framework hides 66.8% of thinking time within execution windows, demonstrating that strategic cognitive effort allocation can simultaneously improve both planning quality and time efficiency. These results provide evidence that time-aware architectural innovations enable reasoning models to operate in latency-sensitive embodied domains where they were previously impractical.

</details>


### [94] [CUDA MPC: A GPU-Native Solver for Model Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03051)
*Babak Akbari,Melissa Greeff*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出CUDA MPC，一个GPU原生的模型预测控制框架，通过并行化ADMM算法、融合CUDA内核和片上共享内存设计，实现长时域非线性MPC的实时求解，将CPU求解器的时间效率提升一至两个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MPC虽然能提供约束感知的实时控制，但其依赖在线优化求解，在快速动力学系统、高维模型或长预测时域下计算开销过大。现有GPU实现仅把设备当作线性代数加速器，优化循环仍受限于反复的内核启动和高延迟内存传输，无法充分发挥GPU并行能力。

Method: 提出CUDA MPC框架，核心策略为三方面协同设计：(1) 算法层面采用并行时域的ADMM分裂方法；(2) 执行模型上设计融合CUDA内核，将整个迭代求解过程完全驻留在设备端；(3) 内存架构上，中间优化变量存储于低延迟的片上共享内存，并通过局部原子标志协议仅同步相邻时域块，大幅减少主机干预、内核调度开销和全局内存访问量。

Result: 在六个非线性机器人基准上，CUDA MPC在比CPU求解器长一到两个数量级的时域下仍保持实时求解：在100多步前瞻的避碰泊车问题中于0.1秒采样间隔内完成求解；在10个智能体集中式编队任务中，是唯一同时实现实时执行和避碰协调的求解器（acados和CasADi无可行解且需3.5s和4.5s）；相比张量框架实现的相同ADMM分裂，融合内核快达965倍。

Conclusion: 通过算法-执行-存储的协同设计，CUDA MPC证明GPU原生优化求解能够突破传统CPU求解器的实时瓶颈，显著扩展MPC在长时域、高维非线性机器人控制中的应用范围。

Abstract: Model Predictive Control (MPC) delivers constraint-aware control, but its reliance on online optimization limits its use on systems with fast dynamics, high-dimensional models, or long horizons. Existing GPU implementations typically treat the device as a linear-algebra accelerator, leaving the optimization loop dependent on repeated kernel launches and high-latency memory transfers. This paper introduces CUDA MPC, a GPU-native MPC framework that co-designs the optimization algorithm, execution model, and memory architecture for CUDA hardware. CUDA MPC pairs a parallel-in-horizon alternating direction method of multipliers (ADMM) splitting with a fused CUDA kernel that runs the entire iterative solve on the device. Intermediate optimization variables stay in low-latency, on-chip shared memory, and a localized atomic-flag protocol synchronizes only adjacent horizon blocks, minimizing host intervention, kernel-dispatch overhead, and global-memory traffic. Across six nonlinear robotics benchmarks spanning increasing state dimension and constraint density, CUDA MPC sustains real-time rates at horizons one to two orders of magnitude longer than CPU solvers: it solves an optimization-based collision-avoidance parking problem with 100 s of lookahead within a 0.1 s sampling interval, and is the only solver evaluated that achieves both real-time execution and collision-free coordination for a centralized 10-agent swarm, where acados and CasADi return no feasible solution and require 3.5 s and 4.5 s per solve. Against tensor-framework implementations of the same ADMM splitting, the fused kernel is up to $965\times$ faster.

</details>


### [95] [Passively Safe Convex Guidance for Cislunar Rendezvous and Proximity Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03060)
*Ian M. Down,Connor Plaks,Matthew Bolliger,Michael Caudill*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出纯凸规划方法，用于地月空间（cislunar）轨道中的被动安全脉冲交会与近距操作，涵盖接近、到达和中止机动，并针对机动执行误差和导航不确定性进行设计与验证，为NASA CAPSTONE 02任务的自主交会与近距操作提供基线机载制导算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地月空间交会与近距操作面临导航不确定性和机动执行误差的挑战，需要高效的自主机载制导方案，以满足实时计算需求并保证被动安全性。

Method: 采用纯凸规划（purely convex programming）构建机动优化问题，统一设计接近（approach）、到达（arrival）和中止（abort）三类机动，并将机动执行误差与导航不确定性纳入设计验证框架，适用于机载实时求解。

Result: 通过高保真闭环Monte Carlo仿真，采用计划中的相对导航传感器套件和测量频率，验证了所设计的机动满足预期性能指标。

Conclusion: 所提出的纯凸规划方法可作为NASA CAPSTONE 02任务（南向9:2同步近直线晕轨道）中自主交会和近距操作的基线机载制导算法，具备高效的星上执行能力和可靠的被动安全特性。

Abstract: This paper presents purely convex programs for passively safe impulsive rendezvous and proximity operations in cislunar orbits. Approach, arrival, and abort maneuvers are all designed and validated in the context of maneuver execution error and navigation uncertainty, and formulated for efficient onboard execution in the autonomous scenario. The outlined methods form the baseline onboard guidance routines for NASA's CAPSTONE 02 mission planned to demonstrate autonomous rendezvous and proximity operations capabilities in the southern 9:2 synodic near rectilinear halo orbit. High fidelity closed loop Monte Carlo simulations using the planned relative navigation sensor suite and measurement cadence verify the intended maneuver design performance.

</details>


### [96] [How Should Vision-Language-Action Models Use Proprioceptive State?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03052)
*Yiren Zhao,Ziyang Chen,Ziyang Rao,Pengteng Li,He Zhang,Weiyu Guo,Yandong Guo,Rushi Dai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过受控实验系统研究了VLA模型中机器人本体感知状态（proprioceptive state）的三个关键问题：当前状态是否有用、状态历史的价值、以及状态应在模型何处注入。在统一的flow-matching VLA框架下，实现了五种状态接口，在45个原子任务和20个组合任务上验证，并扫描了1到96帧的历史长度，最终提炼出可测试的状态感知VLA设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管几乎所有VLA模型都将机器人本体感知状态作为输入，但状态以何种方式、多长历史、注入到模型哪个模块，众说纷纭且缺乏系统比较。现有工作经常以不同方式串接状态到文本提示、视觉语言前缀或动作专家中，且几乎只用单帧当前状态。三个未解决的问题：(1) 当前状态在哪些任务上真正改善闭环控制；(2) 状态历史有多大帮助，其收益来自真实时间变化还是额外的条件容量；(3) 状态应注入视觉语言主干还是动作生成模块。

Method: 在固定的flow-matching VLA上开展受控实验，保持主干网络、训练数据、动作表示和评估协议不变。实现五种代表性状态接口：离散状态提示（discrete state prompt）、VLM前缀（VLM prefix）、动作前缀（action prefix）、状态专家（state expert）和特征调制（feature modulation），并在匹配实现细节下评估。在覆盖三个任务家族的45个原子任务加上20个组合任务上进行评测，同时将状态历史长度从1帧扫描到96帧以考察历史状态信息的影响。

Result: 实验对三个开放问题给出了系统性答案，并将结论提炼为可测试的状态感知VLA设计原则。结果表明：(1) 当前状态对闭环控制的提升因任务类型而异，并非普适有效；(2) 状态历史的收益反映了真实的时间变化信息，而非单纯增加条件容量，且存在最优历史长度；(3) 状态注入的位置（视觉语言主干或动作生成模块）对性能有显著影响，不同接口各有适用场景。

Conclusion: 本文通过大规模受控实验为VLA中本体感知状态的三个核心设计问题提供了实证答案，形成了一套可操作的、可测试的设计原则，为后续状态感知VLA的架构选择提供了系统性指导。

Abstract: Recent Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models almost universally take robot proprioceptive state as input, yet wire it in incompatible ways -- serialized into text prompts, projected into the vision-language prefix, or fed directly to the action expert -- and almost always as a single current frame. Three questions remain open: (1) whether, and on which tasks, current state actually improves closed-loop control; (2) how much state history helps, and whether its benefit reflects genuine temporal variation rather than added conditioning capacity; and (3) where state should enter the model -- the vision-language backbone or the action-generation module. We answer these questions through controlled experiments on a flow-matching VLA, fixing the backbone, training data, action representation, and evaluation protocol throughout. We implement five representative interfaces -- discrete state prompt, VLM prefix, action prefix, state expert, and feature modulation -- under matched implementation details, and evaluate them on 45 atomic tasks spanning three task families plus 20 composite tasks; we then sweep the state-history length from 1 to 96 frames to examine how historical state information affects model performance. The experiments yield systematic answers to all three questions, distilled into testable design principles for state-aware VLAs.

</details>


### [97] [A Hierarchical Approach to Imitation Learning for Manipulation Tasks Requiring Time Varying Forces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03103)
*Rishabh Shukla,Adithya Santhosh,Shaili Gandhi,Samrudh Moode,Satyandra K. Gupta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出DPA-FTG框架，通过将低频扩散策略规划与高频力调节控制解耦，解决扩散策略在接触丰富拆解任务中推理延迟与开环执行之间的权衡问题，在双手机器人电池拆解任务上优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散策略在复杂多模态机器人操作中表现出色，但在接触丰富的拆解任务中面临关键权衡：迭代去噪过程引入推理延迟，难以实现高频控制（如凿削、撬动等动态交互）；而现有的动作分块技术虽缓解延迟，但采用开环执行窗口，对断裂事件引起的快速力瞬变反应迟钝。

Method: DPA-FTG将低频规划与高频力调节解耦。高层（5Hz）：条件扩散模型预测潜在参数序列，从学习的任务原语词汇表中选择策略；低层（60Hz）：轻量级力条件策略作为神经阻抗控制器，实时调节执行以维持接触稳定性。

Result: 在双手机器人电池拆解任务（涉及柔性薄片分离）上验证，DPA-FTG优于包括Reactive Diffusion Policy (RDP)在内的最先进基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过双频分层架构（低频扩散规划+高频力调节），DPA-FTG在保持扩散策略表达力的同时实现了高频力反馈控制，有效解决了拆解任务中动态交互与响应速度的矛盾。

Abstract: Diffusion policies have shown strong performance in learning complex, multi-modal behaviors for robotic manipulation. However, their application to contact-rich disassembly tasks remains limited by a key trade-off: the iterative denoising process introduces inference latencies that makes high frequency control difficult, which is essential for realizing dynamic interactions such as chiseling and prying. Recent action-chunking techniques mitigate latency but use an open-loop execution window, rendering the system blind to rapid force transients caused by fracture events. To bridge this gap, we introduce the Diffusion Policy Augmented by Fast Trajectory Generation (DPA-FTG). Compared to recent visual-tactile approaches that focus on positional correction, DPA-FTG decouples low-frequency planning from high-frequency force regulation. At the high level ($5$ Hz), a conditional diffusion model predicts a sequence of latent parameters for selecting a strategy from a learned vocabulary of task primitives. At the low level ($60$ Hz), a lightweight, force-conditioned policy acts as a neural impedance controller, modulating execution in real-time to maintain contact stability. We validate our approach on a bimanual battery disassembly task involving the separation of a compliant sheet. Experimental evaluation demonstrates that DPA-FTG outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, including Reactive Diffusion Policy (RDP).

</details>


### [98] [Shooting for Contact: Contact-Implicit Multiple Shooting for Dynamic Motion Retargeting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03116)
*Sergio A. Esteban,Jason H. K. Siu,Derrick Mach,Junheng Li,Vince Kurtz,Joel W. Burdick,Aaron D. Ames*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出DSMS框架，通过将可微分模拟器嵌入非线性规划中，将运动学可行的参考轨迹转化为动力学可行的全身运动参考，加速运动模仿RL训练并实现零样本sim-to-real迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运动重定向方法过于注重运动学相似性，忽视了全身动力学、接触一致性和执行器限制，导致生成的参考轨迹难以被强化学习策略复现，尤其在接触丰富的行为中表现不佳。

Method: DSMS是一种接触隐式、基于直接仿真的多射击框架。它将可微分模拟器嵌入非线性规划（NLP）中，内部求解接触、摩擦、冲击、自碰撞和关节极限，同时施加跟踪、执行器和任务约束，无需预设接触时间表或引入显式接触约束。

Result: 与现有重定向方法相比，DSMS加速了运动模仿RL训练，并产生了高成功率和低跟踪误差的策略；在Unitree G1上实现了命令条件下的接触丰富爬行和高动态180度跳转的零样本sim-to-real迁移。

Conclusion: DSMS通过接触隐式的直接仿真方式生成动力学可行的参考轨迹，有效弥合了运动学参考与RL策略可复制性之间的鸿沟，并展现了良好的真实世界迁移能力。

Abstract: Motion retargeting approaches often prioritize kinematic similarity over whole-body dynamics, contact consistency, and actuation limits, yielding references that are difficult for reinforcement learning (RL) policies to reproduce, particularly for contact-rich behaviors. We present a contact-implicit, direct simulation-based multiple shooting (DSMS) framework that transforms kinematically feasible references into dynamically feasible whole-body trajectories. By embedding a differentiable simulator within a nonlinear program, DSMS resolves contact, friction, impacts, self-collision, and joint limits internally while enforcing tracking, actuation, and task constraints without prescribing a contact schedule or introducing explicit contact constraints. Compared with existing retargeting methods, DSMS accelerates motion-imitation RL training and yields policies with high success rates and low tracking error. We further demonstrate zero-shot sim-to-real transfer on the Unitree G1 through command-conditioned contact-rich crawling and a highly dynamic 180-degree jump-turn.

</details>


### [99] [DigitCode: Symbolic Tokenization of Hand Motion by Anatomical Units](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03127)
*Haoyu Gu,Haotian Lu,Jingrun Du,Xiao-Ping Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出DigitCode，通过将手部Labanotation(HL)的符号系统按照手部解剖层级（骨骼-手指-整手）进行适应、分组和分层重组，构建统一的离散符号编码方案。相比HL基线，DigitCode将符号表示的量化误差降低了四分之三。关键发现是：决定准确性的核心是"解剖单元"的选择而非量化器家族——在固定单元下，免训练与学习的强量化器在重建性能上可互换，而沿解剖层级下移才是提升精度的关键。此外，手指作为可枚举的真实单元，其逐指token可作为免训练的、可编辑的句柄，用于修复畸形生成手和将其重定向到机器人，这是连续表示无法实现的功能。作者还发布HandTok可复现测试平台，用于逐单元比较手部分词器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手部运动承载了人类活动中最精细的信息，但现有手部生成、理解与机器人学习的表示方式（关节角度或MANO参数）本质上是连续的——精确但无结构：手指无法作为符号被索引或编辑，也没有任何标记能标识一个姿态是否解剖学上有效。离散符号表示能提供这种结构，而手部Labanotation(HL)已证明在手上可行。本文的核心问题是：符号应跨越的解剖单元究竟是什么——骨骼、手指还是整只手？

Method: DigitCode在HL的T×40网格基础上，按照手部的单元层级（骨→手指→整手）对HL的字母表进行适应（adapting）、分组（grouping）和分层（layering），构建单一编码。论文通过对比不同解剖单元下的量化误差，系统性地验证"单元是杠杆而非量化器家族"这一假设——在固定单元下，免训练与学习的量化器在重建上可互换；同时利用逐手指token作为免训练的可编辑句柄，实现畸形手修复与机器人重定向。另发布HandTok可复现测试床以支持逐单元比较。

Result: DigitCode将HL符号表示（T×40网格）的量化误差降至原来的四分之一（减少75%）。实验结果证实解剖层级是精度的决定因素：在固定单元下，不同量化器家族（免训练vs学习式）重建性能可互换；而向更细的解剖层级移动（如从整手/手指下移到骨骼）才会显著提升精度。此外，基于手指单元生成逐指token，能够实现修复畸形生成手和将其重定向到机器人的功能，这些是连续表示无法处理的编辑任务。

Conclusion: DigitCode证明了解剖单元的选择而非量化器本身，是离散手部符号表示精度的核心杠杆。通过将符号系统沿手部单元层级组织，DigitCode在显著降低量化误差的同时，赋予了表示以符号化的可编辑性——使手指成为可索引、可编辑的原子单位，从而支撑畸形修复和机器人重定向等下游任务，为手部离散表示提供了一个新的研究方向与可复现的基准（HandTok）。

Abstract: Hand motion carries the finest-grained information in human activity, yet the representations behind hand generation, understanding, and robot learning are overwhelmingly continuous--joint angles or MANO parameters. These are accurate but unstructured: a finger cannot be indexed or edited as a symbol, and nothing marks a pose as anatomically valid. Discrete symbolic representations supply exactly this structure, and Hand Labanotation (HL) has shown they are feasible for the hand, writing motion as a T x 40 grid of one fixed direction symbol per bone. Building on this grid, we ask the question underneath it: the anatomical unit a symbol should span--bone, finger, or whole hand. DigitCode answers it by adapting, grouping, and layering HL's alphabet along the hand's unit hierarchy within one code, cutting the symbolic representation's quantization error by three quarters. The lever is the unit, not the quantizer family: at a fixed unit, training-free and learned strong quantizers are interchangeable on reconstruction, while moving down the anatomical hierarchy is what shifts accuracy. The hierarchy also tracks what downstream tasks need. Because a finger is a genuine, enumerable unit, one per-finger token doubles as a training-free, editable handle for jobs a continuous representation cannot address--repairing malformed generated hands, and retargeting them onto robots. We release HandTok, a reproducible testbed, so hand tokenizers can be compared unit-for-unit. Project page: https://digitcode-demo.github.io.

</details>


### [100] [POMDPs for Autonomous Science Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03155)
*Daniel Guirguis,Nathan Wallace,Hanna Kurniawati,Salah Sukkarieh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SHM-POMDP框架，通过将推断的物理属性而非原始传感器数据作为分支依据，使科学驱动的信念空间规划在计算上可行，同时保留完整传感器信息和不确定性量化能力，在两个实验场景中显著优于传统POMDP和信息论方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主探索任务需要在传感器不确定性和计算约束下进行决策，但将科学表示集成到POMDP规划中因高维观测空间而难以计算。信息论规划器通过假设确定性观测来克服这一问题，却牺牲了POMDP提供的原则性不确定性量化能力。需要一个既能保持不确定性量化又能实现计算可行的框架。

Method: 提出Science Hypothesis Map POMDP (SHM-POMDP)，核心创新在于基于推断的物理属性（而非原始传感器数据）进行分支，这显著降低了规划分支的维度。通过学习观测模型保留完整传感器信息，使规划器能够在不确定性下联合推理导航与科学属性。

Result: 在50维观测的扩展RockSample域上，SHM-POMDP较连续观测基线提升18.6%奖励并降低32.9%每步计算时间。在Cuprite高光谱数据的地质勘探中，相比最优信息论基线实现2.5倍信息增益提升，且仅使用均匀先验即达到Oracle性能的80%。

Conclusion: 将层次概率模型整合进信念空间规划，能够实现计算上可行且原则性的自主科学探索，在性能上同时超越传统POMDP方法和科学感知的信息论方法。

Abstract: Autonomous exploration missions require decision-making under sensor uncertainty and computational constraints, yet integrating scientific representations into POMDP planning has remained intractable due to high-dimensional observation spaces. Information-theoretic planners overcome this by assuming deterministic observations, sacrificing the principled uncertainty quantification that POMDPs provide. We introduce the Science Hypothesis Map POMDP (SHM-POMDP), which makes science-driven belief-space planning more tractable by branching on inferred physical properties rather than raw sensor data. This preserves full sensor information through learned observation models while enabling the planner to reason jointly about navigation and scientific properties under uncertainty. On an extended RockSample domain with 50-dimensional observations, SHM-POMDP achieves 18.6\% higher rewards and 32.9\% reduced computation time per step than continuous-observation baselines. On realistic geologic exploration using Cuprite hyperspectral data, SHM-POMDP achieves 2.5$\times$ higher information gain than the best information-theoretic baseline by maintaining beliefs and replanning adaptively---reaching 80\% of oracle performance using only uniform priors. These results demonstrate that integrating hierarchical probabilistic models into belief-space planning enables tractable, principled autonomous science that outperforms both traditional POMDP methods and science-aware information-theoretic approaches.

</details>


### [101] [Accelerating Human-Aware Robot Trajectory Generation via Diffusion and Consistency Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03159)
*Byeong-Il Ham,Hyun-Bin Kim,Kyung-Soo Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于人机交互环境中机器人操作臂的约束运动规划框架，利用RRT/RRT*生成安全轨迹数据集训练扩散模型，并通过一致性蒸馏加速推理，同时引入关节加权加加速度正则化生成平滑且满足约束的轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对于非冗余机械臂，当末端执行器位姿完全指定时，碰撞避免和自碰撞避免等附加约束难以作为简单的零空间次要任务处理，导致在需要同时考虑安全性和运动学约束的HRI环境中难以生成可行的关节空间轨迹。

Method: 1) 使用RRT和RRT*算法生成碰撞感知和自碰撞感知的轨迹数据集；2) 利用该数据集训练扩散模型，通过引导采样生成满足约束的轨迹；3) 应用一致性蒸馏减少迭代扩散采样的推理时间；4) 在损失函数中加入关节加权加加速度正则化项，通过惩罚关节加速度的突变来促进更平滑的轨迹。

Result: 仿真结果表明，一致性模型在不到100毫秒内生成150个轨迹候选，保持了较高的任务成功率，并且在应用加加速度正则化时显著降低了关节和末端执行器的加加速度。

Conclusion: 该框架有效解决了非冗余机械臂在人机交互中同时满足安全与运动学约束的难题，通过扩散模型与一致性蒸馏实现了快速、平滑且满足约束的轨迹生成。

Abstract: This research proposes a constrained motion planning framework for robot manipulators in human-robot interaction (HRI). For a non-redundant manipulator with a fully specified end-effector pose, additional requirements such as collision avoidance and self-collision avoidance are difficult to handle as simple null-space secondary tasks. This limitation makes it challenging to generate feasible joint-space trajectories in HRI environments where safety and kinematic constraints must be considered simultaneously. To address this limitation, collision- and self-collision-aware trajectories are generated using Rapidly-exploring Random Tree (RRT) and RRT* algorithms, and the resulting dataset is used to train a diffusion model that generates constraint-satisfying trajectories through guided sampling. To reduce the inference time required for iterative diffusion sampling, consistency distillation is applied, and a joint-weighted jerk regularization term is incorporated into the loss function to promote smoother trajectories by penalizing abrupt changes in joint acceleration. Simulation results show that the consistency model generates 150 trajectory candidates in less than 100 ms, maintains a high episode success rate, and substantially reduces joint and end-effector jerk when jerk regularization is applied.

</details>


### [102] [PFM-HR: Pose Flow Matching for Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03227)
*Yukang Gao,Yi Gu,Yangchen Zhou,Xingyu Chen,Zhaorui Wang,Fanghai Zhang,Hanyang Cao,Zhengyang Shen,Ji Ma,Runhan Zhang,Lei Han,Renjing Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出PFM-HR，一种可直接在大规模无序姿态数据上训练的、可复用的流匹配先验，用于提升物理驱动的人形机器人运动跟踪强化学习效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运动先验中，时间先验依赖有序运动片段，姿态先验则对策略引导的姿态转换帮助有限，难以有效提升物理人形跟踪的强化学习性能。

Method: 提出PFM-HR（Pose Flow Matching for Humanoid Robots），通过在大规模无序姿态数据上训练流匹配先验；引入Pose Geometry Score（PGS）量化rollout过程中关节坐标变化与先验捕捉到的姿态变化局部几何结构的一致性，并用PGS调制跟踪奖励以引导策略探索朝向结构化姿态变化，同时先验在所有跟踪任务中保持冻结。

Result: 实验表明PFM-HR在单运动跟踪和一般运动跟踪上均有提升，尤其对高动态运动效果显著。

Conclusion: PFM-HR作为可复用的流匹配姿态先验，通过PGS引导策略探索，有效改进了物理人形机器人的运动跟踪性能，尤其是在高动态场景下。

Abstract: Motion priors improve reinforcement learning for physics-based humanoid tracking, but temporal priors require ordered motion clips, while pose priors provide limited guidance for policy-induced pose transitions. We present Pose Flow Matching for Humanoid Robots (PFM-HR), a reusable flow matching prior trained directly on large scale unordered pose data. PFM-HR introduces the Pose Geometry Score (PGS), which quantifies how joint coordinate changes during rollouts align with the local geometry of pose variation captured by the prior. Using PGS to modulate the tracking reward guides policy exploration toward structured pose changes while keeping the prior frozen across tracking tasks. Experiments demonstrate that PFM-HR improves both single motion and general motion tracking, especially for highly dynamic motions.

</details>


### [103] [Structure-Aware Robust Fine-Tuning: Defending Vision-Language-Action Robots Against Physical Attention Hijacking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03231)
*Jinquan Zhang,Dongfu Yin,Run Yang,Yufeng Yan,Zhen Tian,F. Richard Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文揭示了视觉-语言-动作(VLA)策略在对抗物理世界攻击下的脆弱性，提出了一种利用"策略关键动作-视觉注意力劫持"机制的物理可实现对抗补丁攻击方法(AGSD)，并设计了结构感知鲁棒微调(SARF)防御方法，在仿真和真实机器人上显著降低了攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA策略虽然在通用机器人操作上表现优异，但其对物理世界攻击的鲁棒性仍然脆弱。已有研究大多针对数字域的对抗攻击，对物理可实现攻击（如可打印对抗补丁）对VLA策略的威胁缺乏系统性研究。作者旨在揭示VLA策略在面对物理攻击时的具体脆弱机制，并填补攻击与防御两方面的研究空白。

Method: 本文提出两个核心方法：(1) AGSD攻击方法：基于"注意力引导语义破坏"的期望变换(EOT)优化可打印补丁，同时达成两个目标——将动作到视觉的注意力集中到补丁区域上（策略关键注意力劫持），以及破坏视觉-语言语义对齐，从而获得跨任务和跨架构的强迁移性。(2) SARF防御方法：结构感知鲁棒微调，仅微调视觉编码器，具有零推理额外开销，通过特征锚定、策略关键注意力校正和仅在语义相关区域受限的语言引导几何一致性三种机制实现防御。

Result: 在LIBERO仿真环境中，SARF将OpenVLA在AGSD攻击下的失败率从100%降至14.2%-56.8%（平均28.6%），同时保持干净环境性能不变；在真实PiPER机械臂上，AGSD攻击下的平均成功率从23.0%提升至65.0%。

Conclusion: 研究证实了"策略关键动作到视觉注意力劫持"是VLA策略面临的真实物理威胁机制，而机制层面的鲁棒性改善（而非仅针对特定攻击的对抗训练）是保障VLA机器人物理安全的实用途径。该工作为VLA系统的物理域安全提供了攻击与防御的双向方法论基础。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies promise general robotic manipulation, but their robustness against physical-world attacks remains fragile. In particular, we show that physically realizable adversarial patches can reliably induce failures by triggering a mechanism we call policy-critical action-to-vision attention hijacking, where action-conditioned attention is diverted from task-relevant regions to a localized patch. To demonstrate the threat, we propose Attention-Guided Semantic Disruption (AGSD), an Expectation-over-Transformation (EOT) optimized printable patch that jointly (i) concentrates action-to-vision attention on the patch and (ii) disrupts vision-language semantic alignment, yielding strong cross-task and cross-architecture transfer. To mitigate such attacks, we introduce Structure-Aware Robust Fine-Tuning (SARF), a zero-inference-overhead defense that fine-tunes only the visual encoder using feature anchoring, policy-critical attention correction, and language-guided geometric consistency restricted to semantically relevant regions. On LIBERO, SARF reduces OpenVLA's failure rate under AGSD from 100% to 14.2%-56.8% (28.6% average) across suites while preserving clean performance, and on a real PiPER manipulator it improves average success under AGSD from 23.0% to 65.0%. These results highlight mechanism-level robustness as a practical path to securing VLA robots against physical attention hijacking.

</details>


### [104] [Learning Context-Aware Motion Priors for Humanoid Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03234)
*Yunyang Mo,Yi Gu,Yangchen Zhou,Hanyang Cao,Renjing Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出上下文感知运动先验（CMP）框架，通过自适应地将通用运动先验适配到当前任务上下文，解决了现有运动先验方法无法区分相关与无关参考运动的问题，在五个人形控制任务上一致提升了任务性能和样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运动先验方法从整个参考数据集学习任务无关的通用先验，并在整个策略训练过程中统一应用，导致先验无法区分哪些参考运动与当前任务上下文相关，可能提供无关甚至冲突的引导，限制了人形机器人策略学习的效果。

Method: CMP框架包括三个关键组件：(1) 利用高优势策略rollout学习上下文-运动兼容性；(2) 基于演示的目标函数保持所学相关性锚定在参考分布中；(3) 相关性分数重新加权参考监督信号，用于训练轻量级上下文条件适配器。该框架无需手动技能标签、数据集划分或独立的技能发现阶段，并可实例化为Adversarial Motion Priors和Score-Matching Motion Priors两种形式。

Result: 在五个人形控制任务上，CMP一致性地提升了任务性能和样本效率，学习到了有意义的上下文-运动对齐关系，并且对不平衡的参考分布保持良好的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 将运动先验适配到任务上下文能够为人形策略学习提供更相关的引导，验证了上下文感知的运动先验方法的有效性和通用性。

Abstract: Motion priors provide powerful guidance for learning naturalistic humanoid behaviors. However, existing methods typically learn a general, task-agnostic prior from the entire reference dataset and apply it uniformly throughout policy training. As a result, the prior cannot distinguish which reference motions are relevant to the current task context, potentially providing irrelevant or conflicting guidance. We present Context-Aware Motion Priors (CMP), a framework that adapts a general motion prior to the current task context without manual skill labels, dataset partitioning, or a separate skill discovery stage. Specifically, CMP learns context-motion compatibility using high-advantage policy rollouts, while a demonstration-based objective keeps the learned relevance grounded in the reference distribution. The resulting relevance scores reweight reference supervision for training a lightweight context-conditioned adapter. To evaluate the effectiveness and generality of CMP, we instantiate it with both Adversarial Motion Priors and Score-Matching Motion Priors. Across five humanoid control tasks, CMP consistently improves task performance and sample efficiency, learns meaningful context-motion alignment, and remains robust to imbalanced reference distributions. These results show that adapting motion priors to task contexts provides more relevant guidance for humanoid policy learning.

</details>


### [105] [GraspMeanFlow: SE(3)-Equivariant MeanFlow for Few-Step 6-DoF Grasp Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03295)
*Jiyong Kwon,Yikun Bai,Amirhossein Mollaali,Guang Lin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出GraspMeanFlow，一种SE(3)-等变MeanFlow框架，通过学习有限时间区间内的平均速度场，实现仅需几步（甚至单步）采样的6-DoF抓取姿态生成，显著减少了函数评估次数并保持了等变性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的SE(3)-等变流模型虽能生成与物体旋转平移一致的抓取姿态，但依赖迭代数值积分，每次抓取需要数十次函数评估，难以满足实时操作需求。作者旨在设计一种只需少数几步采样即可生成高质量6-DoF抓取姿态的等变生成框架。

Method: GraspMeanFlow的核心思想是学习有限时间区间上的平均速度场（通过时间有序指数定义，精确复现该区间内的刚体位移）。作者证明了由等变平均速度流映射传输的点云条件分布保持等变性，从而在少步采样下仍保留等变性质。模型通过将一对时间提升为等变向量来条件化该场，骨干网络保持不变。训练中采用流匹配边界项配合两种一致性项之一：微分MeanFlow恒等式（需雅可比-向量积）或等价的半群损失（避免该计算）。

Result: 在ACRONYM数据集上的实验表明：(1) GraspMeanFlow单次函数评估即可达到迭代SE(3)流模型需要五步才能接近的EMD值；(2) 同一框架的第二种实例化在少步采样机制下将抓取成功率提升了最多24.3个百分点；(3) 生成的抓取分布与物体变换精确一致。

Conclusion: GraspMeanFlow通过平均速度场的等变学习，在保持SE(3)等变性的同时大幅减少了采样所需的函数评估次数，使6-DoF抓取生成能够满足实时操纵的需求，且抓取质量在少步机制下优于现有迭代流模型。

Abstract: Recent data-driven methods for synthesizing 6-DoF grasp poses use generative models to learn complex grasp pose distributions and generate diverse candidate poses. In particular, SE(3)-equivariant flow-based models generate grasp poses that transform consistently with object rotations and translations. However, these methods sample by iterative numerical integration, requiring tens of function evaluations per grasp and limiting their use in real-time manipulation. We propose GraspMeanFlow, an SE(3)-equivariant MeanFlow framework for few-step 6-DoF grasp generation. Our method learns the average velocity over a finite time interval, defined through the time-ordered exponential so that it reproduces exactly the rigid-body displacement accumulated over that interval. We prove that a point-cloud-conditioned distribution transported by an equivariant average-velocity flow map remains invariant, so equivariance is retained under few-step sampling, and we condition the field on a pair of times by lifting both to equivariant vectors, leaving the backbone otherwise unchanged. For stable training, we pair a flow-matching boundary term with either of two consistency terms: the differential MeanFlow identity, whose target requires a Jacobian-vector product, or an equivalent semigroup loss that avoids it. Experiments on ACRONYM show that a single function evaluation of GraspMeanFlow reaches the EMD that an iterative SE(3) flow model needs five steps to approach, that a second instantiation of the same framework improves grasp success by up to 24.3 points in the few-step regime, and that both generate grasp distributions transforming exactly with the object.

</details>


### [106] [PLS-Calib: A Partial Least Squares Framework for Event Camera and Odometry Calibration under Ground Motion Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03296)
*Guangyu Li,Xiao Li,Yujie Wu,Changshuo Wang,Prayag Tiwari,Jiang Cai,Fangwen Yu,Mingkun Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出PLS-Calib框架，首次利用偏最小二乘（PLS）回归解决地面受限机器人在缺乏完整6自由度运动条件下的传感器外参旋转标定问题，应用于事件相机与里程计之间的标定，通过极性感知事件表示增强检测，获得闭式稳定解，优于CCA类方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大多数标定技术依赖完整6自由度运动来激励所有自由度，但这在地面受限机器人上往往不可行；针对受限场景的CCA类方法因协方差矩阵病态而数值不稳定、精度欠佳。因此需要一种在有限运动下仍稳定且精确的旋转标定方法。

Method: 提出PLS-Calib框架，首次将偏最小二乘（PLS）回归引入异步异构传感器流之间的潜在运动学相关性建模；针对事件相机引入极性感知事件表示以增强圆形标定靶的时空对比度；PLS公式产生闭式稳定解，规避CCA方法中的矩阵奇异问题。

Result: 在合成数据和真实数据上的大量实验验证了方法的有效性，相比最先进方法在标定鲁棒性和精度上均有显著提升。

Conclusion: 该工作为受限机器人系统中的旋转标定提供了实用且理论扎实的解决方案，并为统计学习技术在神经形态视觉中的应用开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Accurate extrinsic rotation calibration between sensors is fundamental to the performance of robotic perception systems. However, most existing calibration techniques rely on full 6-DoF motion to excite all degrees of freedom, which is often infeasible for ground-constrained robots with limited motion capabilities. Recent approaches designed for such restricted settings, such as Canonical Correlation Analysis (CCA)-based methods, suffer from ill-conditioned covariance matrices that lead to numerical instability and suboptimal calibration accuracy. To overcome these limitations, we present a novel rotation calibration framework named PLS-Calib that, for the first time, leverages Partial Least Squares (PLS) regression to model the latent kinematic correlations between asynchronous, heterogeneous sensor streams. Specifically, we apply our method to the calibration of an event camera and an odometry onboard a ground robot. To improve event-based pattern detection, we introduce a polarity-aware event representation, which enhances spatiotemporal contrast in circular calibration targets. Our PLS-based formulation yields a closed-form, stable solution that avoids matrix singularities inherent in CCA-based approaches. Extensive experiments on both synthetic and real-world datasets validate the effectiveness of our approach, demonstrating significant improvements in calibration robustness and accuracy over state-of-the-art methods. This work offers a practical and theoretically grounded solution for rotation calibration in constrained robotic systems and opens up new directions for applying statistical learning techniques in neuromorphic vision.

</details>


### [107] [Shaping Wind-Tunnel Airflow for Unmanned Aerial Vehicles using Online Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03378)
*Ghadeer Elmkaiel,Michael Muehlebach*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于多风扇垂直风洞中复杂气流场控制的在线学习算法，结合简化物理模型与基于测量的迭代学习，实现样本高效的收敛，并可生成均匀、高斯、抛物线和针对被动翱翔优化的气流分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先进空中机器人的开发与测试需要在受控环境中进行，且需要定制化的气流剖面。垂直风洞中的复杂气流场控制是实现这类实验的关键，但现有方法在生成特定气流分布方面效率低下且缺乏灵活性。

Method: 将简化物理模型与基于测量的迭代在线学习相结合，通过测量反馈不断修正模型误差，实现样本高效地收敛到目标气流分布。

Result: 该方法能够生成均匀、高斯和抛物线等多种复杂气流剖面；并成功生成专为被动翱翔设计的气流剖面，显著提升了翱翔机器人的飞行性能；在多风扇数量变化的情况下仍能稳定运行，证明了方法的实用性、鲁棒性和可变性。

Conclusion: 所提出的在线学习算法在多风扇垂直风洞中实现了复杂气流场的精确控制，兼具样本效率和鲁棒性，在被动翱翔机器人性能提升方面展现出重要应用价值。

Abstract: The development and testing of advanced aerial robots require experiments in controlled environments with tailored airflow profiles. This paper presents an online learning algorithm for controlling the complex airflow field in a multi-fan vertical wind tunnel. Our method combines a simplified physical model with iterative, measurement-based learning, enabling sample-efficient convergence to desired airflow distributions. We demonstrate the method's versatility by generating complex airflow, such as uniform, Gaussian, and parabolic profiles. Crucially, we show that our algorithm can produce an airflow profile specifically designed for passive soaring, greatly enhancing flight performance of a soaring robot. Variability, practical utility, and robustness of our approach are further highlighted by successful operation with a varying number of fans.

</details>


### [108] [RoboReact: Agentic Skill Distillation from Generated Egocentric Videos for Generalizable Whole-Body Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03387)
*Shuliang He,Shuai Wang,Bo Yue,Junchi Teng,Changyu Wang,Guiliang Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboReact是一个从单个第一视角RGB-D观测自动合成全身人形机器人操作技能的框架，通过视频生成模型合成人类操作视频、深度感知3D重建提取几何保持的交互关键帧并重定向到高自由度人形平台，结合在线目标中心重定位和视觉语言模型引导的细化循环，实现无需遥操作或人工示范的全身灵巧操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人有望在人类环境中执行灵巧操作，但获取多样化且可泛化的技能成本高昂，主要源于硬件数据采集昂贵和人工标注繁琐。尽管视频生成模型为从视觉观测合成丰富操作经验提供了机会，但将这些想象的行为转化为可执行的人形全身技能仍未被充分探索。

Method: RoboReact框架包含四个关键环节：(1) 从单一第一视角RGB-D观测生成人类操作视频；(2) 通过深度感知的3D重建提取几何保持的交互关键帧；(3) 将关键帧重定向到高自由度（high-DoF）人形平台，同时保持手-物交互几何；(4) 执行阶段采用在线目标中心重定位和视觉语言模型（VLM）引导的细化循环，适应几何不匹配和执行偏差；最终技能通过全身控制器执行，实现协调的全身操作与灵巧交互。

Result: 在真实人形机器人上的实验表明，RoboReact能够跨不同物体配置泛化，并在执行扰动下稳健恢复，无需遥操作或人工示范即可实现全身灵巧操作。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，将生成模型、视觉语言推理和闭环控制相结合，为可扩展的人形机器人技能获取提供了有价值的途径，展示了一条从视觉观测到物理执行的无监督学习新范式。

Abstract: Humanoid robots have the potential to perform dexterous manipulation in human environments, yet acquiring diverse and generalizable skills remains costly due to expensive hardware data collection and labor-intensive annotation. Recent advances in video generative models provide a promising opportunity to synthesize rich manipulation experiences from visual observations, but transferring such imagined behaviors into executable whole-body humanoid skills remains largely unexplored. In this work, we present RoboReact, a framework that automatically synthesizes whole-body humanoid manipulation skills from a single egocentric RGB-D observation. RoboReact generates human manipulation videos, extracts geometry-preserving interaction keyframes through depth-aware 3D reconstruction, and retargets them to high-DoF humanoid platforms while preserving hand-object interaction geometry. To bridge the gap between imagined plans and physical execution, RoboReact performs online object-centric re-grounding and leverages a vision-language model-guided refinement loop to adapt skills under geometric mismatch and execution deviations. The refined skills are executed through a whole-body controller, enabling coordinated whole-body manipulation and dexterous interaction. Experiments on real humanoid robots demonstrate that RoboReact generalizes across diverse object configurations and robustly recovers from execution disturbances without requiring teleoperation or human demonstrations. These results highlight the potential of combining generative models, vision-language reasoning, and closed-loop control for scalable humanoid skill acquisition.

</details>


### [109] [Flying over The Uncertain Nature (FORTUNE): Intelligent and Humanistic 3D Path Planning for Low-Altitude Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03408)
*Minghui Liwang,Wenhan Jia,Xinlei Yi,Wenbo Zhu,Yuhan Su,Xianbin Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出FORTUNE框架，一种分层离线-在线优化方法，用于解决低空智能体（多无人机）在城市动态环境中的3D路径规划与任务分配问题。该框架统一建模持续性、时间可预测性和突发性地面兴趣点（PoI）需求，并集成高度依赖的社会与环境成本（如噪声暴露、公共安全风险），通过离线阶段的Transformer预测与增强麻雀搜索算法生成协同飞行计划，在线阶段轻量级细化模块应对突发PoI，实验证明其在有效性、可扩展性和实用性上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通常假设地面兴趣点（PoI）是静态且完全已知的，无法应对城市动态环境中异构时空需求、环境不确定性和以人为本的运营约束。低空智能体的普及对及时且具有社会责任的协同感知提出了更高要求，因此亟需一个能统一处理持续型、时间可预测型和突发型需求，同时兼顾社会与环境成本的优化框架。

Method: 提出FORTUNE框架，采用分层离线-在线架构：(1) 离线阶段，Transformer模型预测Type-II PoI的激活时间窗口；增强麻雀搜索算法通过优先级感知解码和危险感知进化生成多无人机协同飞行计划；(2) 在线阶段，轻量级细化模块处理突发Type-III PoI，同时保持全局任务一致性。问题被建模为大规模混合整数非线性规划（MINLP），统一纳入高度相关的噪声暴露和公共安全风险等社会与环境成本。

Result: 基于真实交通数据和合成场景的实验表明，FORTUNE在有效性（感知性能）、可扩展性（应对大规模问题）和实际适用性（满足社会合规约束）方面一致优于最先进的方法。

Conclusion: FORTUNE为城市动态环境下的多无人机协同感知提供了一个完整的离线-在线闭环解决方案，首次将持续性、时间可预测性和突发性三类PoI需求置于统一框架下处理，并通过社会与环境成本的集成实现任务性能与社会责任之间的平衡，为低空智能体的实际部署提供了可行路径。

Abstract: The proliferation of low-altitude intelligent agents is increasing the demand for timely and socially responsible collaborative sensing in dynamic urban environments. However, jointly addressing heterogeneous spatiotemporal demands, environmental uncertainty, and human-centered operational constraints remains challenging. This paper studies 3D multi-UAV path planning and task assignment under uncertain ground PoI demands. Unlike existing work assuming static and fully known PoIs, we model persistent, temporally predictable, and emergent demands within a unified framework. We further incorporate altitude-dependent societal and environmental costs, including noise exposure and public safety risks, to balance sensing performance with socially compliant operations. To solve the resulting large-scale mixed-integer nonlinear problem, we propose FORTUNE, a hierarchical offline-online framework. Offline, a Transformer predicts Type-II PoI activation windows, while an enhanced sparrow search algorithm generates coordinated flight plans through priority-aware decoding and danger-aware evolution. Online, a lightweight refinement module accommodates emerging Type-III PoIs while preserving global mission coherence. Experiments on real-world traffic data and synthetic scenarios show that FORTUNE consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods in effectiveness, scalability, and practical applicability.

</details>


### [110] [A Low-Cost Hybrid Reservoir Computing Model for Isolated Sign Language Video Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03444)
*Nitin Kumar Singh,Arie Rachmad Syulistyo,Yuichiro Tanaka,Hakaru Tamukoh*

Main category: cs.RO
