TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于轻量级储层计算（RC）的手语识别（SLR）方法，利用MediaPipe提取身体和手部关键点，通过混合储层计算（HRC）架构（结合深度储层计算DRC和双向储层计算BRC）进行高维动态表征，再以岭回归映射到类别标签。在WLASL100数据集上取得了与深度学习方法相当的准确率，同时训练时间大幅缩短至数秒，适合边缘设备部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习在手语识别中表现优异，但其高计算成本限制了在边缘设备上的部署，因此需要一种计算开销低且性能可接受的手语识别方法。

Method: 1) MediaPipe提取身体和手部关键点，捕捉手势的空间和时间动态；2) 混合储层计算（HRC）架构结合深度储层计算（DRC）和双向储层计算（BRC），将输入转换为高维动态表示；3) 岭回归模型将最终HRC状态映射为类别标签。

Result: 在WLASL100视频数据集上，Top-1、Top-5和Top-10准确率分别为61.12%、86.05%和92.56%，与深度学习方法性能相当；训练时间从DL方法的数分钟/数小时级骤减至仅数秒。

Conclusion: 该方法具有低计算成本、快速训练的特点，展示了在手语识别中部署于边缘设备的潜力。

Abstract: Sign language recognition (SLR) enhances communication between hearing and hearing-impaired individuals. Although deep learning (DL) has achieved promising performance in SLR, its high computational cost limits deployment on edge devices. To address this challenge, we propose a lightweight reservoir computing (RC)-based approach for SLR. In the proposed method, MediaPipe extracts body and hand keypoints to capture the spatial and temporal dynamics of gestures. These keypoints are then processed by a hybrid reservoir computing (HRC) architecture that combines deep reservoir computing (DRC) and bidirectional reservoir computing (BRC), transforming the input into a high-dimensional dynamic representation. A ridge regression model maps the final HRC state to class labels. This HRC-based SLR method achieved Top-1, Top-5, and Top-10 accuracies of 61.12%, 86.05%, and 92.56%, respectively, on the Word-Level American Sign Language 100 (WLASL100) video dataset, demonstrating competitive performance compared to deep learning-based approaches. Additionally, due to the lightweight nature of RC, the training time was drastically reduced to only a few seconds compared with DL-based methods such as Bi-GRU.This method offers low computational cost, showing its potential for deployment on edge devices.

</details>


### [111] [Continue or Replan? Bernoulli-Continuation Policy Learning for Adaptive Horizon Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03483)
*Weichen Xu,Zhenhua Liu,Lin Luo,Yaobo Liang,Chengtang Yao,Qingyu Mei,Jian Cao,Xixin Cao,Xing Zhang,Jiaolong Yang,Baining Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Bernoulli-Continuation Policy (BCP)，一种轻量级即插即用的自适应执行框架，通过将固定长度动作块的执行时域选择分解为一系列继续或重新规划的二值决策，并利用轨迹级强化学习训练，从而克服了现有chunk-based VLA模型周期性重规划与任务进度无关的缺陷。在RoboTwin 2.0上将平均成功率提升11.08%（低成功率任务）及4.06%（全部50任务），并在真实机器人、不同基础策略和LIBERO基准上均展现显著泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于chunk的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在执行固定数量动作后才进行重规划，这是一种与任务进度无关的任务无关周期性调度。当重规划边界未落在关键操作阶段之前时，该阶段将基于过时的chunk执行而非使用重新规划的新chunk，导致性能受限。

Method: 提出BCP框架：1) 保持基础VLA冻结，仅增加一个轻量级"延续头"（continuation head）；2) 将执行时域选择从独立类别的分类问题转化为前缀共享的序数归纳偏置的序列二值决策（继续或重规划）；3) 由于最优执行时域不可观测，采用从轨迹级结果出发的强化学习训练；4) 设计重规划效率奖励（Replanning-Efficiency Reward），同时奖励任务成功和高效VLA使用，防止策略崩溃为过短执行时域。

Result: 在RoboTwin 2.0上：低成功率13个任务平均成功率提升+11.08%，全部50任务从89.88%升至93.94%（+4.06%）；仅Clean设置训练即可泛化到Randomized设置（+4.06%）；跨基础策略迁移到π0.5，在LIBERO提升+1.7%，在更难LIBERO-PRO提升+6.8%；真实机器人两个操作任务成功率从74%→92%和44%→84%；开销可忽略且因成功率提升，总体运行时间低于固定时域基线。

Conclusion: BCP通过轻量的即插即用设计，在不修改基础VLA的情况下实现了自适应执行时域，显著提升了任务成功率和VLA使用效率，且具有良好的跨设置、跨策略和真实场景泛化能力。其可忽略的计算开销加上性能提升，使得整体运行时间反而低于固定时域基线，验证了自适应重规划策略在VLA框架中的有效性与实用性。

Abstract: Existing chunk-based Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models execute a fixed number of actions (i.e., execution horizon) before replanning, turning replanning into a task-agnostic periodic schedule that is independent of task progress. As a result, when no replanning boundary falls before a critical manipulation stage, it is executed from a stale chunk rather than a freshly replanned one. To address this limitation, we propose Bernoulli-Continuation Policy (BCP), a lightweight, plug-and-play framework for adaptive horizon execution that keeps the base VLA frozen. Given a fixed-length action chunk, its continuation head decomposes execution-horizon selection into a sequence of continue-or-replan decisions, which imposes an ordinal, prefix-sharing inductive bias over candidate horizons rather than treating them as independent classes. Since the optimal horizon for each chunk is not observable, we train this head with reinforcement learning from trajectory-level outcomes and introduce a Replanning-Efficiency Reward that jointly rewards task success and efficient VLA usage, discouraging the policy from collapsing to unnecessarily short horizons. On RoboTwin 2.0 with LingBot-VLA as the base policy, BCP improves the average success rate by +11.08% on 13 low-success tasks and from 89.88% to 93.94% (+4.06%) across all 50 tasks. Although trained only under the Clean setting, BCP generalizes to the Randomized setting, raising the average success rate by +4.06%. It also transfers to a different base policy $π_{0.5}$, achieving a better result on LIBERO (+1.7%) and, notably, on the harder LIBERO-PRO (+6.8%). On a real robot, BCP lifts success from 74% to 92% and from 44% to 84% on two manipulation tasks. Meanwhile, its negligible overhead, combined with higher success, makes BCP's overall runtime even lower than the fixed-horizon baselines.

</details>


### [112] [Lightweight 3D Object Detection via Mamba-Based Knowledge Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03490)
*Quoc Cuong Ninh,Huy Xuan Pham,Anh Tung Nguyen,Dinh Hoan Trinh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于知识蒸馏的轻量级3D LiDAR目标检测框架，通过选择性体素空间特征对齐，将强教师模型（基于Mamba的多分支骨干网络）的对象级体素表示迁移至轻量学生模型，在显著降低计算开销的同时保持与最先进方法相当的检测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于LiDAR的3D目标检测方法通常采用复杂架构提取特征并整合大量上下文信息以提升精度，但这导致计算开销巨大，在资源受限的嵌入式设备（如自动驾驶车载平台）上性能欠佳。需要在精度与计算效率之间寻求更好的平衡。

Method: 提出了一种知识蒸馏框架，包含三个关键组件：(1) 基于选择性状态空间模型（Mamba）的多分支Mamba教师骨干网络，用于提取强特征表示；(2) 盒感知特征迁移机制，用于对齐教师和学生网络之间空间对应的体素特征；(3) 基于Mamba的投影模块，实现选择性体素空间特征对齐，将对象级体素表示从教师迁移至轻量学生模型。

Result: 在公开数据集和真实世界数据上的实验结果表明，该方法在显著降低计算负载的同时，与最先进方法相比保持了具有竞争力的检测精度。

Conclusion: 本研究验证了通过知识蒸馏结合线性时间序列模型（Mamba）进行体素级特征对齐的有效性，为在计算资源受限的嵌入式设备上实现高效且准确的3D LiDAR目标检测提供了一种可行方案。

Abstract: 3D object detection using light detection and ranging (LiDAR) sensors requires a balance between accuracy and computational efficiency for onboard perception in autonomous driving and robotic navigation. Many existing LiDAR-based detection methods employ complex architectures to extract features, integrating large amounts of contextual information to enhance accuracy. This often results in significant computational costs, leading to suboptimal performance on resource-constrained embedded devices. In this study, we propose a knowledge distillation framework that transfers object-level voxel representations from a strong teacher model to lightweight student models through selective voxel-space feature alignment. Taking advantage of the linear-time sequence model with selective state spaces (Mamba), we design a multi-branch Mamba teacher backbone and a box-aware feature transfer mechanism that aligns spatially corresponding voxel features between teacher and student networks through a Mamba-based projection module. Experimental results on both a public dataset and real-world data show that our approach significantly reduces computational load while maintaining competitive accuracy compared with state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [113] [Principles of Robot Autonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03496)
*Daniele Gammelli,Joseph Lorenzetti,Katie Luo,Gioele Zardini,Marco Pavone*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 这是一本关于自主机器人系统的综合性教科书，基于斯坦福大学数十年的教学经验，统一介绍了现代自主系统堆栈的核心方法，将经典机器人与现代物理AI相结合，并配有Jupyter笔记本与实践练习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着自主机器人从实验室走向现实世界（道路、空中、仓库和太空），机器人自主性已从纯学术追求转变为实践者依赖的成熟技术方法集合。然而，缺乏一个统一的、聚焦于实际部署的、能够桥接经典机器人与现代物理AI的系统性教材来指导该领域的学习者。

Method: 本书基于斯坦福大学数十年的教学积累，在一个统一的概念框架内发展现代自主系统堆栈（autonomy stack）的核心要素，将经典机器人与现代物理AI相结合。每个主要主题都配有动手操作的Jupyter笔记本和以实现为导向的练习，使读者在建立理论理解的同时获得实践直觉。

Result: 该书提供了一种有原则、易获取且以部署为导向的自主系统基础，可作为学生、工程师和研究人员进入自主系统这一快速增长领域的综合资源。

Conclusion: 该书为设计、分析或贡献下一代自主系统的人提供了坚实的理论基础，并通过实践练习确保了从理论到实际部署的连贯学习路径。

Abstract: Autonomous robots are moving rapidly from research labs into everyday life - on roads, in the air, in warehouses, and in space. Robot autonomy is no longer solely an academic pursuit, but a collection of mature, field-tested methods and tools that practitioners rely on in real-world deployments. This book offers a clear, unified introduction to the methods that make this possible. Built on decades of teaching at Stanford, the text develops the core elements of modern autonomy stacks within a single conceptual framework, bridging classical robotics and modern physical AI. Every major topic is paired with hands-on Jupyter notebooks and implementation-driven exercises, so readers build practical intuition alongside theoretical understanding. The result is a principled, accessible, and deployment-aware foundation for anyone seeking to design, analyze, or contribute to the next generation of autonomous systems. This is a comprehensive resource for students, engineers, and researchers entering one of today's fastest-growing fields.

</details>


### [114] [Pivot-Centric Trajectory Prediction: Bridging Long Horizons via Dynamical Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03521)
*Xiucong Zhao,Jindong Tian,Hao Miao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出PCTP（Pivot-Centric Trajectory Prediction），通过引入"枢轴点"将长时程轨迹预测任务分解为多个尺度的短期子任务，并解耦为枢轴预测和基于枢轴的轨迹细化两个过程，以缓解长时程预测中的弱引导和误差累积问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着预测时程增长，现有的终点补全（endpoint-completion）和迭代精化（iterative-refine）方法面临弱引导（weak guidance）和误差累积（compounding errors）的挑战，难以实现准确的长时程运动预测。

Method: PCTP将长时程轨迹预测解耦为两个过程：1）枢轴预测过程，利用全局地图上下文和智能体间交互来识别枢轴点；2）基于枢轴的轨迹细化过程，聚焦局部地图细节并根据预测的枢轴点细化短期轨迹。该方法可作为灵活模块集成到大多数现有SOTA轨迹预测模型中。

Result: 在Argoverse I和Argoverse II数据集上，PCTP显著提升了多个领先模型的预测精度，且对模型大小影响极小。PCTP与QCNet结合后在Argoverse II排行榜上超越了所有已发表的ensemble-free方法。

Conclusion: PCTP通过枢轴点提供更多中间引导并减少误差累积，是一种灵活且有效的长时程轨迹预测增强方案，能够无缝集成到现有主流模型中并显著提升精度。

Abstract: Forecasting precise future motion of surrounding agents is essential for reliable autonomous vehicles. However, as the demand for longer prediction horizons increases, existing endpoint-completion or iterative-refine methods increasingly struggle with weak guidance and compounding errors. To tackle the long-horizon prediction challenge, we propose Pivot-Centric Trajectory Prediction (PCTP). By introducing ``pivots'' and focusing on predicting pivot points along extended trajectories, we divide the long-term prediction task into short-term sub-tasks at various scales. Specifically, PCTP decouples the long-term trajectory predicting process into two processes: pivot prediction and pivot-based trajectory refinement. The pivot prediction process aims to utilize global map context and agent-to-agent interactions to identify these ``pivot points'', while the pivot-based trajectory refinement process focuses on local map details and refines the short-term trajectory based on predicted ``pivot points''. Compared with existing methods, PCTP provides more intermediate guidance while reducing compounding errors. Moreover, PCTP is a flexible approach that can be integrated into most state-of-the-art trajectory prediction models. Experimental results show that PCTP improves the prediction accuracy of leading models on both Argoverse I and Argoverse II datasets with minimal impact on model size. Specifically, PCTP combined with QCNet outperforms all published ensemble-free methods on the Argoverse II leaderboard at submission.

</details>


### [115] [Tired Actor: Fatigue-Informed Character Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03528)
*Shengyuan Zhang,Xinpeng Liu,Muchun Niu,Yulong Chen,Lizhuang Ma,Yue Gao,Cewu Lu,Yong-Lu Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出将疲劳机制作为有限能量的代理注入物理仿真角色动画中，利用三室控制器（3CC）模型实现不同疲劳状态下的一般运动模仿策略，并系统分析了疲劳对角色动画自然度、可扩展性和鲁棒性的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物理仿真角色动画在模仿广泛运动上已取得显著进展，但大多假设角色拥有无限能量，缺乏生物力学和生理学先验，导致动作不自然。行为能量学研究表明能量消耗塑造人类运动方式，因此引入疲劳作为有限能量限制的代理来改善角色动画的真实感。

Method: 采用疲劳作为有限能量极限的代理，将其注入一般角色动画框架；基于三室控制器（3CC）模型的能量消耗机制，训练能够在不同疲劳状态下进行一般运动模仿的策略。

Result: 成功获得了不同疲劳状态下的一般运动模仿策略，并通过广泛分析展示了疲劳对角色动画自然度、可扩展性和鲁棒性的影响。

Conclusion: 将疲劳机制引入物理仿真角色控制赋予动画更符合人类能量消耗规律的运动特征，提升了自然度，同时为角色动画的扩展性和鲁棒性研究提供了新视角。代码将开源。

Abstract: Replicating human behavior with physics simulation has been a long-expected goal in character animation. Existing efforts have achieved impressive performance in imitating a wide span of general motions. However, most existing efforts could still suffer from unnatural movements due to the lack of biomechanical and physiological priors. Given this, we project our sights to advances in behavioral energetics, which demonstrate how energy use shapes human movements. In contrast, current character controllers typically assume the character is equipped with infinite energy over time. Inspired by these, we propose to adopt fatigue as a proxy of the finite energy limit, inject it into general character animation, and thoroughly investigate how fatigue introduces new characteristics to physics-based character control. Leveraging the Three-Compartment Controller (3CC) model, we managed to obtain a policy for general motion imitation under different fatigue statuses. Furthermore, extensive analyses are conducted to demonstrate how fatigue could influence the naturalness, scalability, and robustness of character animation. Our code will be made public.

</details>


### [116] [Human Centric Embodied Intelligence for Soft Wearable Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03556)
*Rainier Natividad,Raye Chen-Hua Yeow*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出"以人为中心的具身智能（HCEI）"概念和"感知-认知-驱动-增强（PCAA）"框架，重新审视软体可穿戴机器人的设计范式，从传统的执行器优先转向以感知和认知为设计主导，系统综述了从材料、传感、AI、驱动到人机交互、数字孪生、临床转化、制造、法规和伦理等全链条进展，旨在推动下一代软体可穿戴机器人向个性化、预测性和以人为本的智能方向发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体可穿戴机器人已从概念验证阶段迈入实际应用，其核心挑战不再仅是开发更软的材料和更强大的执行器，而是如何将感知、智能和人体适应能力集成到用户能长期舒适穿戴且信任的系统中。现有研究缺乏统一的框架来整合这些跨学科要素，作者提出HCEI和PCAA框架以填补这一空白。

Method: 作者提出PCAA（Perception-Cognition-Actuation-Augmentation）框架作为组织综述的系统性视角，将感知和认知定位为设计的核心驱动力，打破传统以执行器为中心的设计范式。在此框架下综合评述软材料、可穿戴传感、人工智能、驱动技术、人机交互、数字孪生、临床转化、制造、监管和伦理等多维度进展，分析各组件如何协同作用于长期个性化和实际部署。

Result: 该综述系统整合了软体可穿戴机器人领域的跨学科进展，阐明感知和认知应在设计流程中占据主导地位，揭示了各相互依赖组件如何共同塑造长期个性化定制和现实场景部署，并提供了一个统一的PCAA概念框架来指导该领域未来的研究和转化方向。

Conclusion: 通过提出统一的概念框架和设计视角，这项工作旨在引导未来研究方向、促进跨学科合作，并加速下一代软体可穿戴机器人向个性化、预测性和以人为本的智能穿戴系统转化落地。

Abstract: Soft wearable robots have evolved rapidly from proof-of-concept devices into promising platforms for rehabilitation, occupational assistance, and human augmentation. As the field matures, its central challenge extends beyond the development of softer materials and more capable actuators to the integration of sensing, intelligence, and human adaptation into systems that users can wear comfortably, trust, and benefit from over extended periods. This transition motivates the concept of Human-Centric Embodied Intelligence (HCEI), in which intelligence emerges from the coupled human-robot system through the interaction of morphology, multimodal sensing, adaptive cognition, compliant actuation, and the wearer's own physiological and behavioral adaptation. To organize this perspective, this review introduces the Perception-Cognition-Actuation-Augmentation (PCAA) framework, which positions perception and cognition as the primary drivers of design, shifting development beyond the conventional actuator-first paradigm. Using this framework, the review synthesizes advances in soft materials, wearable sensing, artificial intelligence, actuation, human-robot interaction, digital twins, clinical translation, manufacturing, regulation, and ethics, highlighting how these interdependent components collectively shape long-term personalization and real-world deployment. By providing a unified conceptual framework and design perspective, this review aims to guide future research, foster interdisciplinary collaboration, and accelerate the translation of next-generation soft wearable robots toward personalized, predictive, and human-centric wearable intelligence.

</details>


### [117] [Unified Visuomotor Targets: Supervising VLAs Beyond Physical Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03563)
*Zhenyang Feng,Unnat Jain*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出UVT（统一视觉运动目标），一种统一的潜在预测目标，同时编码电机控制和视觉场景转换信息，在不改变架构、不增加数据的情况下，通过改变VLA策略训练时预测的目标（而非架构设计），提升了训练效率、任务性能和策略鲁棒性。在两个代表性VLA系统上的仿真基准和真实双臂操作任务中均验证了有效性，尤其在小训练预算和具有挑战性的环境条件下增益显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型将视觉和语言观测映射为机器人动作，但存在本质不匹配：VLM编码的是丰富、高层级的场景与目标表征，而机器人动作是低层级、任务结构有限的信号。作者质疑：与其改变策略的架构设计，不如改变策略被训练去预测的目标，是否能产生更好且训练更高效的策略。

Method: 提出UVT（Unified Visuomotor Target），一种统一的潜在预测目标，联合编码电机控制信息和视觉场景转换信息。无需架构改动，无需额外数据，直接应用于现有VLA系统，改变训练时的预测目标。

Result: 在仿真基准和真实双臂操作任务中，UVT应用于两个代表性VLA系统，显著提升了训练效率、最终任务性能和策略鲁棒性，尤其在有限的训练预算和具有挑战性的环境条件下，增益尤为突出。

Conclusion: 改变策略的训练预测目标（而非架构设计）是提升VLA策略的有效途径。UVT通过统一编码控制和视觉转换信息，弥合了高层级VLM表征与低层级动作信号之间的鸿沟，实现了更高效、更鲁棒的策略学习。

Abstract: VLA models are trained to predict robot actions from visual and language observations. This is a natural choice, but it creates a mismatch: VLMs encode rich, high-level representations of scenes and goals, while robot actions are low-level signals with limited task structure. We ask whether changing what the policy is trained to predict, rather than how it is architecturally designed, can yield better and more efficiently trained policies. We propose UVT (Unified Visuomotor Target), a unified latent prediction target that jointly encodes motor control and visual scene transition information, requiring no architectural changes and no additional data. Applied to two representative VLA systems across simulation benchmarks and real bimanual manipulation tasks, UVT improves training efficiency, final task performance, and policy robustness, with particularly strong gains under limited training budgets and challenging environmental conditions. Rollout videos and additional qualitative results are available at our project webpage: https://unified-visuomotor-targets.github.io/

</details>


### [118] [Active Stiffness Control of a Supportive Continuum Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03677)
*Rana Danesh,Farrokh Janabi-Sharifi,Farhad Aghili*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对腱驱动支撑性连续体机器人（SCR）提出了一种主动任务空间刚度控制框架，利用投影滑模控制器实现操作臂末端的位置调节，并通过虚拟笛卡尔弹簧实现主动表观刚度控制，以增强机器人抵抗外部载荷的能力和定位鲁棒性。仿真与实验验证表明，增大刚度增益可有效减小负载引起的末端偏转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 支撑性连续体机器人（SCR）通过机械耦合支撑臂来增强操作连续体机器人的承载能力，但其被动刚度由机械构型决定，无法在线调节以适应变化的载荷或交互力，因此需要主动刚度控制来调节负载响应并维持定位精度。此外，闭链结构带来的运动学约束使得任务空间调节和刚度控制复杂化，需要专门的框架来解决这一问题。

Method: 本文提出一个针对腱驱动SCR的主动任务空间刚度控制框架。首先，利用已有的几何变应变模型描述闭链动力学，并将其投影到约束一致的运动子空间上；其次，设计投影滑模控制器来调节操作臂尖端同时保持约束，并通过Lyapunov分析建立闭环稳定性；最后，在位置调节后，基于位置误差反馈引入虚拟笛卡尔弹簧，实现主动表观刚度以塑造力-位移响应。框架在仿真中评估，并在预设外部载荷和不同期望构型下进行实验验证。

Result: 仿真与实验结果表明，增大指令刚度增益可减小负载引起的尖端偏转并增加表观方向刚度，从而提升负载抵抗能力和外部加载下的定位鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 所提出的主动任务空间刚度控制框架能够有效调节腱驱动SCR在外部载荷下的刚度特性，显著改善其负载响应和定位精度，为支撑性连续体机器人在变载荷场景下的应用提供了一种可行的控制方案。

Abstract: Supportive continuum robots (SCRs) enhance the load-bearing capability of an operative continuum robot by mechanically coupling it with a supportive arm. However, their passive stiffness is determined by the mechanical configuration and cannot be adjusted online for varying payloads or interaction forces. Active stiffness control is therefore needed to regulate the load response and maintain positioning accuracy. Meanwhile, the closed-chain structure introduces kinematic constraints that complicate task-space regulation and stiffness control. This paper presents an active task-space stiffness control framework for a tendon-driven SCR. An existing geometric variable strain model describes the closed-chain dynamics, which are projected onto the constraint-consistent motion subspace. A projected sliding mode controller regulates the operative arm tip while preserving the constraints, and closed-loop stability is established through Lyapunov analysis. After position regulation, active apparent stiffness is introduced through a virtual Cartesian spring based on position-error feedback to shape the force--displacement response. The framework is evaluated in simulation and experimentally validated under prescribed external loads and different desired configurations. Results show that increasing the commanded stiffness gain reduces load-induced tip deflection and increases apparent directional stiffness, thereby improving load resistance and positioning robustness under external loading.

</details>


### [119] [LiLa-WAM: Lightweight Latent Reasoning World-Action Model for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03701)
*Fan Yang,Yuting Su,Xiaobo Wang,Yuncheng You,Fugui Fan,Yuting Wu,Minghui Wu,Chenxu Zhao,JiaHong Ning,Peiguang Jing*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LiLa-WAM是一种轻量级世界-动作模型，在紧凑的潜空间中推理未来状态，可在单个24GB GPU上端到端训练，并通过Visual Transition Token实现无语言任务表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的世界-动作模型（WAMs）存在计算开销大的问题：像素空间方法将大量容量分配给与控制无关的视觉细节，而某些潜空间方法需要多阶段训练来构建推理空间，导致在有限计算预算下难以训练。

Method: LiLa-WAM的核心设计是一个由未来状态预测和动作生成共同塑造的紧凑潜推理空间，使模型保持轻量级同时与控制保持对齐。此外提出Visual Transition Token（VTT），一种无语言任务表示，将每个任务编码为视觉特征空间中的一个方向。

Result: 在RoboTwin 2.0、LIBERO和真实机器人任务上验证了有效性，在50个RoboTwin任务上实现90.48%的成功率，且仅需单GPU训练。

Conclusion: LiLa-WAM证明了通过紧凑潜空间推理和轻量级任务表示，世界-动作模型可以在显著降低计算成本的同时保持甚至超越现有方法的性能，为在有限硬件预算下训练世界模型提供了可行方案。

Abstract: World-action modeling has emerged as a promising paradigm for robotic control, as it empowers models to go beyond reacting to observations and anticipate how a scene will evolve. However, existing WAMs often incur substantial computational overhead. Pixel-space methods often allocate substantial capacity to visual details that may not be directly relevant to control, while some latent-space methods require multi-stage training to construct the reasoning space. The resulting training cost can make such methods difficult to train under modest computational budgets. In this work, we propose LiLa-WAM, a lightweight world-action model that reasons about the future in a compact latent space and can be trained end-to-end on a single 24GB GPU. Its core design is a compact latent reasoning space jointly shaped by future-state prediction and action generation, which keeps the model lightweight while remaining well aligned with control. For task specification, we further propose the Visual Transition Token(VTT), a language-free task representation that encodes each task as a direction in visual feature space. Experiments on RoboTwin~2.0, LIBERO, and real-robot tasks demonstrate LiLa-WAM's effectiveness, achieving 90.48\% success across 50 RoboTwin tasks with single-GPU training.

</details>


### [120] [Track4Action: Distilling World-Centric 3D Tracker into Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03727)
*Chenyi Wang,Xinkai Wang,Bokai Lin,Jialin Tian,Fucheng Zhang,Cewu Lu,Lixin Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Track4Action框架，利用冻结的3D跟踪器将演示片段中的动作-世界变化对应关系蒸馏到VLA策略中，在无需跟踪器部署的情况下显著提升机器人操作性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动作标签只告诉VLA策略模仿哪些机器人命令，但未说明这些命令如何改变3D世界。而演示片段中的帧转换包含了几何、运动、可见性和相机变化的监督信息，这些丰富的"实现转换"信息尚未被充分利用。

Method: Track4Action包含两个核心组件：Track4World在训练时将演示片段V_{t:t+K}编码为池化跟踪器特征；可学习的跟踪查询从当前VLA隐状态推断该特征，在共享空间中匹配，并通过特征级门控条件化流匹配动作头。跟踪器特征仅用于定义对齐目标，部署时不需要片段或跟踪器。

Result: 在零样本LIBERO-Plus上达到82.3%，比无对齐变体高7.6个百分点，比LaMP高3.0个百分点；在RoboTwin 2.0的干净和随机分裂上分别获得80.44%和81.48%；在四个物理双臂任务上平均成功率67.5%，比无对齐变体高25.0个百分点。

Conclusion: 跨仿真和物理任务的性能提升表明，动作对齐的3D跟踪器特征可作为特权监督，支持无跟踪器部署的VLA策略实现更好的操作表现。

Abstract: Action labels tell a vision-language-action (VLA) policy which robot commands to imitate, but not how those commands change the 3D world. The aligned demonstration clip contains this missing supervision because its $K$ frame transitions record the geometry, motion, visibility, and camera change produced during the corresponding $K$ actions. We introduce Track4Action, a framework that distills this realized transition from a frozen world-centric 3D tracker into a current-observation VLA policy. During training, Track4World encodes the clip $V_{t:t+K}$ into a pooled tracker feature. Learnable track queries infer this feature from current VLA hidden states, match it in a shared space, and condition a flow-matching action head through a feature-wise gate. The tracker feature only defines the alignment target, so neither the clip nor the tracker is used at deployment. Track4Action reaches 82.3% on zero-shot LIBERO-Plus, improving the alignment-free variant by 7.6 points and LaMP by 3.0 points. It obtains 80.44% and 81.48% on the clean and randomized RoboTwin 2.0 splits, and 67.5% average success across four physical bimanual tasks, 25.0 points above the alignment-free variant. The gains across simulation and physical tasks support action-aligned 3D tracker features as privileged supervision for tracker-free VLA deployment. Our project page is available at https://wing0night.github.io/track4action-project-page.

</details>


### [121] [GORDON: Graph-based Object-centric Rewards for Decomposition of Long-Horizon Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03753)
*Andrea Protopapa,Davide Buoso,Francesca Pistilli,Georgia Chalvatzaki,Giuseppe Averta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出GORDON框架，基于图神经网络的自监督物体中心奖励学习方法，从无动作视频演示中学习密集奖励，通过活动感知加权池化对齐任务语义，并利用时序奖励轮廓自动发现子任务，在MAGICAL和ManiSkill3基准的七个操作任务上实现74.4%的平均成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时序操作技能强化学习面临奖励设计复杂、稀疏奖励指导有限、人工子任务标注成本高等挑战。虽然视觉演示可用于奖励学习，但从原始像素学习的奖励对视觉变化、背景外观和机器人运动敏感且脆弱。因此需要一种更鲁棒、物体中心且能自动分解长时序任务的奖励学习方法。

Method: GORDON框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 将每个视觉场景表示为检测物体及其空间关系的图结构，用GNN以自监督方式嵌入到任务对齐的潜在空间；(2) 引入活动感知加权池化机制，强调任务相关物体同时掩盖以机器人为主体的运动；(3) 奖励定义为当前状态与演示目标配置在潜在空间中的距离。在长时序任务中，利用奖励的时间轮廓揭示阶段式物体状态转换，实现无人工标注的自动子任务发现，并训练子任务特定奖励和策略进行顺序组合。

Result: 在MAGICAL和ManiSkill3基准的七个操作任务上，物体中心奖励提升了短时序设置的强化学习效果；在复杂长时序任务中，通过自动分解实现了成功的策略学习，平均成功率达到74.4%，相比最优学习基线平均提升约35个百分点，相比oracle方法平均提升约25个百分点。

Conclusion: GORDON验证了物体中心图表示结合自监督学习能有效克服像素级奖励的脆弱性，且通过奖励时序轮廓自动发现子任务可成功应对长时序操作学习中的复杂性问题，无需人工子任务标注。

Abstract: Learning long-horizon manipulation skills with reinforcement learning remains challenging due to the complexity of reward design, the limited guidance of sparse rewards, and the high cost of manual subtask annotation. Visual demonstrations can provide supervision for reward learning, but rewards learned from raw pixels can be brittle and sensitive to visual variation, background appearance, and robot motion. In this work, we propose GORDON, a graph-based object-centric reward learning framework that learns dense rewards from action-free video demonstrations. Each visual scene is represented as a graph of detected objects and spatial relations, and a graph neural network is trained in a self-supervised manner to embed these graphs into a task-aligned latent space. To align the representation with semantic task progress, we introduce an activity-aware weighted pooling mechanism that emphasizes task-relevant objects while masking robot-dominated motion. The dense reward is then computed as distances in the learned latent space of the current state to demonstrated goal configurations, providing a measure of task progress. In long-horizon tasks, the temporal profile of this reward reveals stage-wise object-state transitions, enabling automatic subtask discovery without manual segmentation. The discovered segments are then used to train subtask-specific rewards and specialized policies that are composed sequentially. Experiments on seven manipulation tasks on MAGICAL and ManiSkill3 benchmarks show that our object-centric reward improves reinforcement learning in short-horizon settings and enables successful policy learning in complex long-horizon tasks through automatic decomposition, achieving an average success rate of 74.4% across the long-horizon tasks (on average approximately +35 p.p. vs. best learned baseline and approximately +25 p.p. vs. oracle).

</details>


### [122] [Design and Evaluation of an AI-Enabled Cloud-Edge Architecture for Connected Precision Agriculture Farms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03816)
*Deckshitha Angadi,Koteshwar Goud Surga,Nagaraju Lakkaraju,Naveena Budda,Vikas Agarwal,Giridhara Venkata Ram Raj Mulasa,Ravi Killamsetty,Chandrasekhara Sarma Mallubhotla,Narsimlu Kemsaram*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于AI的云边协同架构，融合IoT传感器、无人机、深度学习、Azure IoT Hub云分析和多平台接口（移动端、Web端和嵌入式边缘设备），用于番茄病害的实时检测，实验检测准确率约92-95%，可在多样环境和设备平台上保持一致性表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 番茄作物极易遭受早疫病、晚疫病和叶霉病等病害侵袭，造成重大产量损失。人工监测仅适用于小规模农场，在大规模场景下难以管理，亟需一种自动化、可扩展的作物病害监测方案。

Method: 提出AI赋能的云边端架构，集成IoT传感器、无人机（UAVs）、深度学习模型、Azure IoT Hub云分析和多平台接口（移动应用、Web应用和嵌入式边缘设备平台）。使用PlantVillage和Kaggle等公开数据集进行训练与验证，基于TensorFlow构建模型并部署到移动、Web和边缘设备平台，实现实时番茄病害检测。

Result: 实验结果表明该系统的检测有效率达到92-95%，在多样环境和不同设备平台上保持一致的性能表现。系统提高了病害检测效率，降低了对人工检查的依赖。

Conclusion: 所提出的系统能够实现及时干预，支持可持续的互联精准农业农场，为大规模作物病害自动化监测提供可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Plant diseases cause significant yield losses worldwide, with tomato crops particularly susceptible to early blight, late blight, and leaf mold. Manual monitoring is practical only for small-scale farms and becomes unmanageable at larger scales. To tackle this limitation, an artificial intelligence (AI) enabled cloud-edge architecture is proposed for autonomous crop monitoring. This proposed architecture integrates Internet of Things (IoT) sensors, unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs), deep learning, Azure IoT Hub-based cloud analytics, and multi-platform (mobile app, web app, and embedded edge device platform) interfaces to enable real-time detection of tomato diseases. For training and validation, we used publicly available datasets, such as PlantVillage and Kaggle. A TensorFlow model trained on a collected dataset is deployed across mobile, web, and edge-device platforms. Experimental results show detection effectiveness around 92-95%, with consistent performance over diverse environments and device platforms. The proposed system improves disease detection effectiveness, lowers dependence on manual inspection, and enables prompt interventions, thereby supporting sustainable, connected precision agriculture farms.

</details>


### [123] [Designing Social Robots for Inclusive Child Wellbeing Assessment: Insights from Communities Supporting Developmental Language Disorder and Forced Migration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03820)
*Fethiye Irmak Dogan,Yue Lou,Alva Markelius,Emma Geijer-Simpson,Gustaf Gredebäck,Tamsin Jane Ford,Ginevra Castellano,Hatice Gunes,Georgina Warner,Jenny L. Gibson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究通过设计探针和焦点小组访谈，探讨如何为具有不同沟通需求的儿童（包括发育性语言障碍儿童和被迫移民背景儿童）设计包容、适当且伦理可接受的社交机器人辅助幸福感评估交互，并提出了相应的伦理与包容性设计建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自评问卷对语言理解和语言表达要求较高，给存在沟通障碍的儿童（如发育性语言障碍儿童和被迫移民背景儿童）的幸福感与心理健康评估带来显著挑战。尽管社交机器人被认为是支持幸福感评估的潜在工具，但关于如何为沟通需求多样的儿童设计包容、适当且伦理可接受的交互的研究仍然有限。

Method: 研究者创建了候选的儿童-机器人交互活动作为设计探针，并与支持（或护理）发育性语言障碍儿童和被迫移民背景儿童的家长及专业人士开展了焦点小组讨论，随后通过主题分析提炼关键考量因素。

Result: 主题分析识别出与机器人角色与能力、交互动态、个体差异、儿童自主性相关的考量因素，以及由儿童沟通需求和生存经历塑造的特定人群考量因素。基于这些发现，研究者推导出了一系列伦理且包容的机器人介导幸福感评估设计建议。

Conclusion: 该工作为具有多样沟通需求儿童的包容性机器人介导幸福感评估提供了设计指导，填补了相关研究空白。

Abstract: Assessing children's wellbeing and mental health can be particularly challenging for children experiencing communication barriers, such as children with Developmental Language Disorder (DLD) and children with forced migration backgrounds. During the assessment process, traditional self-report questionnaires place substantial demands on language comprehension and verbal expression. In this context, social robots have emerged as a promising tool for supporting wellbeing assessment without solely relying on self-report questionnaires, yet limited research has examined how such interactions can be designed to be inclusive, appropriate, and ethically acceptable for children with diverse communication needs. To address this gap, we created candidate child--robot interaction activities as design probes and conducted focus groups with parents and professionals supporting children with DLD and children with forced migration backgrounds. Through thematic analysis, we identified considerations relating to robot role and capabilities, interactional dynamics, individual differences, and child agency, alongside population-specific considerations shaped by children's communication needs and lived experiences. Based on these findings, we derive a set of ethical and inclusive design recommendations for robot-mediated wellbeing assessment. By foregrounding these considerations and recommendations, this work contributes design guidance for inclusive robot-mediated wellbeing assessments for children with diverse communication needs.

</details>


### [124] [EvoHIL: Self-Evolving Reward and Flow-Matched Policy Optimization for Robust Human-in-the-Loop Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03872)
*Shuoqin Zhang,Tongtong Cheng,Xiru Gao,Jinzhuo Peng,Bin Zheng,Jiahao Tu,Ke Wang,Jia Pan,Zhe Hu,Kai Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EvoHIL是一个统一的"人在回路强化学习"框架，通过自进化奖励（SER）、动作流稳定（AFS）和保留感知的离线微调三个模块，分别适应奖励模型、动作生成器和视觉域，解决现有HIL-RL在场景变化、时间一致性和外观漂移三方面的耦合局限。在Franka FR3和SO-101机械臂上跨六个操作任务、受控光照变化条件下，相较人在回路和模仿学习基线提升了任务成功率、人类确认标签一致性、运动平滑度和完成时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类在回路强化学习使机器人能够从有限真实交互中学习接触丰富的操作技能，但部署时暴露出三个相互耦合的局限：静态视觉奖励模型在场景变化下失效；独立采样动作导致运动在时间上不一致；基于视觉的策略对外观变化敏感。这些局限阻碍了HIL-RL在真实世界中的可靠部署。

Method: EvoHIL由三个关键组件构成：（1）自进化奖励（SER）：从人类确认的正样本和临时的弱负样本中自适应地进化成功分类器；（2）动作流稳定（AFS）：通过流匹配生成时间上连贯的动作块，将策略更新与已执行动作前缀和示范行为对齐；（3）保留感知的离线微调：在重新渲染的交互数据上回放训练，同时将AFS决策者-批评者锚定到先前的行为上，实现无需额外机器人交互的视觉域适应。

Result: 在六个操作任务上使用Franka FR3和SO-101机械臂，在受控光照变化条件下进行实验，EvoHIL相比人在回路强化学习和模仿学习基线，在任务成功率、与人类确认标签的一致性、运动平滑度和完成时间等指标上均表现更优。

Conclusion: EvoHIL通过分阶段的人机交互学习过程统一地适应奖励模型、动作生成器和视觉域，有效解决了HIL-RL部署中的三方面耦合局限，验证了跨不同机械臂平台和任务的有效性。

Abstract: Human-in-the-loop reinforcement learning (HIL-RL) enables robots to learn contact-rich manipulation from limited real-world interaction, but deployment exposes three coupled limitations: static visual reward models fail under scene changes; independently sampled actions cause temporally inconsistent motion; and vision-based policies remain sensitive to appearance shifts. We present EvoHIL, a unified framework that adapts the reward model, action generator, and visual do main within a staged human-in-the-loop learning process. First, self-evolving reward (SER) adapts the success classifier from human-confirmed positives and provisional weak negatives. Second, Action Flow Stabilization (AFS) generates temporally coherent action chunks through flow matching, grounding policy updates in executed action prefixes and demonstrated behavior. Third, retention-aware offline fine-tuning replays relit interaction data while anchoring the AFS actor-critic to prior behavior, adapting the visual domain without additional robot interaction. Across six manipulation tasks on Franka FR3 and SO-101 arms under a controlled lighting shift, EvoHIL improves task success, agreement with human-confirmation labels, motion smoothness, and completion time relative to human-in-the-loop and imitation baselines.Project page: https://anonymous4366.github.io/EvoHIL/

</details>


### [125] [ETA: A New Agentic Paradigm for Embodied Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03924)
*Yitong Chen,Zezheng Huai,Sixian Li,Yubang Wang,Haozhe Zhang,Yifei Zhang,Hechang Chen,Jingjing Gong,Yu-Gang Jiang,Xipeng Qiu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Embodied Task Agent (ETA)范式，将数字智能体扩展到物理世界，通过Planner-Interface-World循环实现机器人对陌生任务和环境的泛化、长时程可控执行以及从经验中学习，并发布开源实现OpenETA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前具身系统主流采用端到端的观测到动作路径，但泛化能力高度依赖训练数据覆盖度，长任务执行难以控制和检查，远未达到类似ChatGPT让机器人具备通用能力的突破性时刻。

Method: ETA将机器人系统重构为三个核心组件：Planner（每次选择一个工具调用）、Interface（控制执行）和World（返回结果并提供新观测）。该循环允许智能体验证结果、调整计划，并将成功与失败的交互转化为可复用的经验。OpenETA提供可替换的Planner、可组合的Tools和Skills、可审计记忆、可重放轨迹，以及统一的仿真和真实机器人接口。

Result: OpenETA作为开源实现发布，且对于Codex仅需轻量级插件即可接入，只需暴露observe、mark_point和move_to三个接口。

Conclusion: ETA通过引入显式的规划-执行-感知循环范式替代端到端黑箱，使机器人系统具备可验证、可适配、可学习的特性，为通向通用机器人的ChatGPT时刻提供了一条新的路径。

Abstract: When will robots have their ChatGPT moment? Such a breakthrough requires a general-purpose robot that can handle unfamiliar tasks in unfamiliar environments, remain controllable over long interactions, and learn from experience.
  Today's embodied systems largely follow an end-to-end observation-to-action path. Despite rapid progress, they remain far from this goal: their generalization depends heavily on the coverage of robot training data, while long task execution remains difficult to control and inspect. To realize this goal, we introduce the Embodied Task Agent (ETA), a new paradigm for extending digital agents into the physical world, and release OpenETA as its open-source implementation. ETA centers the robot around a Planner that chooses one Tool call at a time, an Interface that controls execution, and a World that returns the result and a fresh observation. This loop allows the agent to verify outcomes, adapt its plan, and turn successful and failed interactions into reusable experience. OpenETA provides replaceable Planners, composable Tools and Skills, auditable memory, replayable trajectories, and common interfaces for simulation and real robots. For Codex, OpenETA can operate as a lightweight plugin that exposes only observe, mark_point, and move_to.

</details>


### [126] [Bimanual Manipulation Within an 8 GB Budget: Zero-Copy Sensing and Quantized ACT on an Entry-Level Jetson](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03938)
*Ekansh Singh,Eva Samuel,Alessandra Reneau,Ryan Schmeelk,Yashvi Gandhi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套在NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano Super（入门级嵌入式GPU）上完全运行的双手操作（bimanual SO-101）系统，仅用桌面GPU（RTX 3070）进行离线训练。系统在可变形豆袋的抓取放置任务上评估了三条优化路径：NVMM零拷贝传感（节省CPU占用和延迟）、ACT与Diffusion Policy的训练收敛对比（ACT收敛而DP未收敛）、以及TensorRT FP16/INT8量化加速（推理延迟降低6.4-9.0倍且任务成功率保持）。还报告了两个ACT新发现：TensorRT INT8校准对ResNet18有效但对全部145个transformer层无效，以及量化的必要性取决于ACT的动作分块配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模仿学习的双手操作策略通常在高端工作站或数据中心级GPU上评估，其在嵌入式硬件上的部署成本（内存占用、延迟、CPU负载）缺乏系统性表征。作者旨在探索将此类策略部署到入门级嵌入式平台（Jetson Orin Nano Super）的可行性与瓶颈，填补这一空白。

Method: （1)构建基于GStreamer和NVMM缓冲的三相机零拷贝采集管线，对比传统拷贝路径在帧率、CPU峰值占用与最差延迟上的差异；（2）在相同演示数据上分别以各自参考预算训练ACT（100k梯度步）和Diffusion Policy（200k梯度步），评估收敛能力；（3）将ACT转换为TensorRT，在FP16和INT8精度下量化并测量推理延迟与任务成功率，同时分析量化层选择情况。

Result: 零拷贝传感使CPU峰值单核占用从98.0%降至77.0%，最差延迟从117.31ms降至101.52ms，且传统路径本身不丢帧。ACT在100k步内收敛至任务胜任水平（19/20次成功率），而Diffusion Policy即使200k步也未能收敛（0/10）。TensorRT FP16将推理延迟从114.02ms降至17.93ms（6.4x），INT8进一步降至12.65ms（9.0x），三种精度任务成功率均保持（19/20、18/20、19/20）。INT8校准量化了ResNet18骨干但拒绝了全部145个transformer层，导致INT8较FP16体积仅减少0.9%但延迟再降28%。此外，ACT在n_action_steps=100时全精度可行，但在per-step再预测的时间集成配置下必须量化。

Conclusion: 入门级嵌入式GPU（Jetson Orin Nano Super）配合合理的工程优化（零拷贝采集、TensorRT量化）可以胜任双手操作策略的实时部署。ACT相比Diffusion Policy在嵌入式部署场景下更具收敛效率和可行性，且INT8量化在ACT上存在架构层面的局限性（transformer层无法被通用INT8校准量化），量化需求与动作分块配置密切相关。

Abstract: Bimanual manipulation policies trained with imitation learning are typically evaluated on workstation or datacenter-class GPUs, leaving the cost of deploying them on embedded hardware largely uncharacterized. We present a bimanual SO-101 system running entirely on an NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano Super (8 GB), the entry-level tier of NVIDIA's embedded line, using a desktop GPU (RTX 3070) only for offline training, evaluated on pick-and-place of a deformable beanbag. First, we build a GStreamer capture pipeline backed by NVMM buffers that removes redundant host-device copies from three-camera sensing. Contrary to expectation, the conventional path fit the memory budget and dropped no frames; what zero-copy sensing recovers is CPU headroom (peak single-core utilization 98.0% to 77.0%) and worst-case latency (117.31 ms to 101.52 ms). Second, we train ACT and Diffusion Policy on identical demonstrations, each at its own reference budget (100k gradient steps for ACT, 200k for Diffusion Policy). ACT converges to a task-competent policy (19/20 trials) while Diffusion Policy does not converge to a usable one (0/10) even at twice the step count, which we attribute to differing convergence costs rather than an accuracy ceiling. Third, we convert ACT to TensorRT. FP16 reduces mean inference latency from 114.02 ms to 17.93 ms (6.4x) and INT8 to 12.65 ms (9.0x), with task success preserved at all three precisions (19/20, 18/20, 19/20). We report two findings not previously documented for ACT: TensorRT's general-purpose INT8 calibration quantizes the ResNet18 backbone but accepts zero of 145 transformer layers, explaining INT8's negligible size reduction over FP16 (0.9%) despite a further 28% latency gain; and the need for quantization is conditional on ACT's action-chunking configuration, feasible in full precision at n_action_steps = 100 but not at the per-step re-prediction temporal ensembling requires.

</details>


### [127] [Stochastic Multiple Shooting Trajectory Optimization via Sequential Local Policy Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03978)
*Ashwin Gupta,Joseph Moore*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种随机多重打靶轨迹优化方法，将短控制序列与局部反馈策略连接，提升采样效率和终端约束收敛性，并可从rollout中近似合成系统雅可比矩阵，适用于黑盒动力学的基于模型的强化学习。在三个非线性欠驱动问题上验证了改进效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机单点打靶轨迹优化方法（如MPPI）能处理概率动力学和无梯度情况，但在长动作序列上满足终端约束时采样效率低，需要大量迭代才能收敛。

Method: 提出随机多重打靶方法，优化通过局部反馈策略连接的短控制动作序列以提升采样效率和终端集收敛；同时从rollout中合成近似系统雅可比矩阵，使方法可用于黑盒动力学的基于模型的强化学习。

Result: 在三个非线性欠驱动优化问题（经典倒立摆摆起、神经网络动力学倒立摆摆起、VTOL四平面高攻角失速着陆）上验证了改进的采样效率和终端集收敛性。

Conclusion: 随机多重打靶轨迹优化相比随机单点打靶方法在采样效率和终端集收敛性方面均有显著提升，且不依赖显式梯度或解析动力学模型。

Abstract: Stochastic single shooting trajectory optimization methods such as Model Predictive Path Integral control (MPPI) have been widely adopted in robotics due to their ability to reason about probabilistic dynamics and provide solutions where model gradients are noisy, costly to evaluate, or unavailable. However, satisfaction of terminal constraints when shooting over long action sequences is often sample inefficient, requiring a large number of iterations for convergence. In this paper, we present a stochastic multiple shooting method that optimizes short control action sequences connected via local feedback policies to improve sample efficiency and convergence to a terminal set. Additionally, we show that we are able to synthesize approximate system Jacobians purely from rollouts, making the method suitable for model-based reinforcement learning with black-box dynamics. We demonstrate the algorithm has improved sample efficiency and terminal set convergence for three nonlinear, underactuated optimization problems: a classic cartpole swingup task with analytical dynamics, a cartpole swingup task with learned neural network dynamics, and a VTOL quadplane performing a high angle-of-attack, precision post-stall landing maneuver.

</details>


<div id='math.NT'></div>

# math.NT [[Back]](#toc)

### [128] [A Classification of Multiply Monogenic Quartic Orders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02983)
*Shabnam Akhtari,Jaxon Shumaker*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究二次单生成四次阶（two-times monogenic quartic orders），证明在任意四次数域中，位于其整数环中且不属于Bérczes、Evertse和Győry定义的两种特定类型的二次单生成Z-阶只有有限多个，从而将此前在Galois群特定条件下的结果推广到所有四次数域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bérczes、Evertse和Győry此前在数域K的伽罗华闭包的伽罗华群满足特定条件下，证明了不是两种特定类型的二次单生成Z-阶只有有限多个。本文旨在将此结果推广到所有四次数域，消除对伽罗华群的限制条件。

Method: 本文针对四次数域进行专门分析，利用四次数域特有的结构性质和相关代数关系，对二次单生成阶进行分类研究，证明在一般四次数域中有限的结论仍然成立。

Result: 证明了对于所有四次数域K，在其整数环中不存在无限多个不属于Bérczes-Evertse-Győry定义的两类特定类型的二次单生成Z-阶。

Conclusion: 本文成功将此前依赖于伽罗华群条件的结果推广到了所有四次数域，填补了该问题的空白。

Abstract: We study two-times monogenic quartic orders; i.e., those of the shape $\mathbb{Z}[α] = \mathbb{Z}[β]$, with algebraic integers $α$ and $β$ not $\mathbb{Z}$-equivalent. Two specific types, describing possible algebraic relation among monogenizers of two-times monogenic orders were defined by Bérczes, Evetrse, Győry, who proved under certain conditions on the Galois group of the normal closure of a given number field $K$, that there can be only finitely many two-times monogenic $\mathbb{Z}$-orders in the ring of integers $K$ which are not of these specific two types. In this article, we prove this fact for all quartic number fields.

</details>


### [129] [Counting in Vieta graphs over $\mathbb{F}_p$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03097)
*Bogdan Nica*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文引入并研究了一类具有代数起源的有限简单图——Vieta图，它定义在有限域F_p上对称多元（每个变量二次）方程的解集上，是对近期文献中广泛研究的Markoff图在F_p上的广义推广。作者给出了一种基于二次特征和的系统方法，用于回答Vieta图的顶点数和度分布等基本计数问题，并针对三维和四维情形给出了显式计数结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Markoff图在有限域上已被深入研究，作者希望将其推广到更一般的一类对称多元二次方程对应的图结构上，从而建立统一的系统理论框架。

Method: 利用二次特征和（quadratic character sums）这一数论工具，系统分析Vieta图的顶点计数与度分布问题，并对低维情形（3变量和4变量）给出显式计数公式。

Result: 在三维和四维变量情形下，给出了Vieta图的顶点数和度分布的显式计数结果。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了Vieta图的一般计数框架，将Markoff图的已知结果推广到更广泛的代数图族，为后续研究更高维情形提供了系统方法。

Abstract: We introduce and study a finite simple graph of algebraic origin: the Vieta graph on the solution set over $\mathbb{F}_p$ to a symmetric, multivariate equation which is quadratic in each variable. This construction is a broad generalization of the Markoff graph over $\mathbb{F}_p$, extensively studied in the recent literature. We give a systematic approach, partly based on quadratic character sums, to the following basic counting questions: how many vertices does a Vieta graph have, and what is the degree distribution? We focus on explicit counts, addressing the low-dimensional cases in three and four variables.

</details>


### [130] [On pairs of primes with small order reciprocity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03268)
*Craig Costello,Gaurish Korpal*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种筛选算法，用于寻找模互质时乘法阶数很小的素数对，这是构造配对友好曲线2-循环的必要条件。通过数据库示例表明，除了一个已知的无限素数族外，随着素数增大，此类实例变得越来越稀少，引出了有趣的开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找具有小乘法阶数的素数对是构造配对友好曲线2-循环的必要条件，而这类曲线在密码学应用中有重要用途。该问题的求解对密码学领域的相关构造具有重要意义。

Method: 提出一种筛选（sieving）算法来搜索满足条件的素数对，即两个素数彼此模对方具有小乘法阶数的特性。

Result: 通过数据库收集到的实例表明，除了一个已知的无限素数族外，随着素数规模增大，满足条件的实例变得越来越稀少。这一发现揭示了该问题的稀疏性。

Conclusion: 该研究指出了关于此类素数对分布的开放问题，并通过构建数据库为后续研究提供了基础，希望引发更深入的探讨。

Abstract: We give a sieving algorithm for finding pairs of primes with small multiplicative orders modulo each other. This problem is a necessary condition for obtaining constructions of $2$-cycles of pairing-friendly curves, which have found use in cryptographic applications. Our database of examples suggests that, except for a well-known infinite family of such primes, instances become increasingly rare as the size of the primes increase. This leads to some interesting open questions for which we hope our database prompts further investigation.

</details>


### [131] [Admissible Pairs: A Variation to Pollock Conjectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03309)
*Anji Dong,Vi Anh Nguyen,Alexandru Zaharescu*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文引入了"可容许对"（admissible pair）的概念，并证明了关于二十面体数和十二面体数的Pollock猜想的一种变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Pollock关于多面体数的猜想（如四角锥数、立方体数等）一直是数论中经典而未完全解决的问题。本文希望推广或变体化Pollock关于二十面体数（icosahedral numbers）和十二面体数（dodecahedral numbers）的猜想，为其提供新的证明途径或更强的结论。

Method: 引入"可容许对"这一新概念，利用该概念建立新的数学框架，从而对Pollock关于二十面体数和十二面体数的猜想进行变体形式的证明。

Result: 成功证明了Pollock关于二十面体数和十二面体数猜想的一个变体版本。

Conclusion: 通过引入可容许对的概念，本文为Pollock猜想提供了新的变体证明，拓展了多面体数理论的研究方法。

Abstract: We introduce a notion of an admissible pair, and prove a variation to Pollock's conjectures on icosahedral and dodecahedral numbers.

</details>


### [132] [Mellin Transform Formulas for Anderson Modules and Hints of Modularity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03326)
*Oğuz Gezmiş,Nathan Green*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文为阿贝尔、可均匀化的Anderson模引入了对数函数的公式，并将其与第二作者提出的动机映射相结合，将Drinfeld模的对偶Goss L-函数的特殊值与相应t-余动机的刚性解析平凡化联系起来。这些公式还被用于将某些L-值与矩阵值模形式的特殊值相关联，推广了Pellarin以及Pellarin和Perkins在秩二情形下的向量值Drinfeld模形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究的动机在于建立Drinfeld模及其相关对象的算术理论中L-函数特殊值与模形式之间的深刻联系，特别是通过Anderson模的对数函数公式这一工具，将解析对象（刚性解析平凡化）与算术对象（L-值、模形式）统一起来，并推广已有的秩二向量值Drinfeld模形式结果到更一般的矩阵值情形。

Method: 作者首先为阿贝尔、可均匀化的Anderson模建立对数函数的显式公式；然后利用第二作者近期引入的动机映射，将这些公式与Drinfeld模的对偶Goss L-函数相联系；通过t-余动机的刚性解析平凡化实现L-值的解析表达；最后将这些结果应用于矩阵值模形式，推广到更一般的高秩情形。

Result: 得到了Anderson模对数函数的显式公式，建立了Drinfeld模对偶Goss L-函数特殊值与t-余动机刚性解析平凡化之间的联系，并将特定L-值与矩阵值模形式的特殊值相关联，在秩二情形下统一推广了Pellarin和Pellarin-Perkins的向量值Drinfeld模形式理论。

Conclusion: 本文通过Anderson模对数函数公式这一关键技术途径，成功搭建了Drinfeld模L-函数特殊值与矩阵值模形式之间的桥梁，为函数域算术中L-函数与模形式理论的交叉融合提供了新的视角和统一框架。

Abstract: In the present paper, we introduce formulas for the logarithm function of abelian, uniformizable Anderson modules. Combining our formulas with a motivic map introduced recently by the second author, we relate special values of dual Goss $L$-functions of Drinfeld modules to the rigid analytic trivialization of their corresponding $t$-comotives. We apply these formulas to relate certain $L$-values to special values of matrix valued modular forms, generalizing the vector valued Drinfeld modular forms of Pellarin and Pellarin and Perkins in the rank two setting.

</details>


### [133] [Erdős-Moser Equation in Arithmetic Progressions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03375)
*Anji Dong,Vi Anh Nguyen,Alexandru Zaharescu*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了等差数列中的 Erdős-Moser 方程，证明了当 k=2 时，等差数列形式的和必须仅包含两项或四项才能存在解，且这两种情况下解均可完全刻画并存在无穷多个。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Erdős-Moser 方程是数论中的经典问题，其一般形式下是否有非平凡解至今未解。作者将该方程推广到等差数列的框架下，旨在探索更一般结构下方程解的存在性与可描述性，从而深化对原问题的理解。

Method: 作者在等差数列的设定下分析 Erdős-Moser 方程，对 k=2 的情形进行专门讨论，通过数论工具（如同余条件、算术级数的构造分析等）确定解的必要条件，进而给出解的完整刻画。

Result: 当 k=2 时，等差数列中的 Erdős-Moser 方程仅在和包含两项或四项时才有解；且两种情形下均存在无穷多个解，且这些解可以被完全地刻画出来。

Conclusion: 本文对 k=2 情形下等差数列中的 Erdős-Moser 方程给出了完整的分类结果，证明了解的存在性仅局限于两项或四项的情形，并提供了解的无穷性及完备刻画。

Abstract: We consider the Erdős-Moser equation $1^k+2^k+\cdots+(m-1)^k=m^k$ in arithmetic progressions. We prove among other things that when $k=2$, for any solution to exist, the above sum in arithmetic progression must consist of two or four terms. In either case, there are infinitely many solutions that can be completely characterized.

</details>


### [134] [Explicit Jordan decompositions for ideal lattices in CM fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03564)
*Guilhem Mureau*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文对CM数域E的分数理想a通过Hermitian迹形式构造理想格，并给出该格在素理想p处Jordan分解的显式公式，其依据是a在E中的素理想因子分解，并通过局部化将其约化为p上方素数的局部行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理想格在算术与密码学中有重要应用，其等距关系的判定需要局域不变量。理解理想格在素理想处的Jordan分解结构，是刻画理想格等距关系和结构化格的关键步骤。

Method: 采用Erez、Morales和Perlis的方法，将整体计算约化到p上方素数的局部行为。通过分析a在E中的素理想因子分解，在p处显式计算由Hermitian迹形式h_{E/F}诱导的理想格的Jordan分解。

Result: 给出了理想格在素理想p处Jordan分解的显式公式，该公式由a在E中的素理想因子分解决定，从而提供了理想格等距关系的局部不变量。

Conclusion: 所得局域不变量可用于研究理想格之间的等距关系，对算术和密码学中出现的结构化格研究具有潜在应用价值。

Abstract: Let $E$ be a CM number field, $F$ a totally real subfield (e.g. $\mathbb Q$), and let $\mathfrak a$ be a non-zero fractional ideal of $E$. Endowed with the Hermitian trace form $h_{E/F}(x,y)=\mathrm{Tr}_{E/F}(x\overline{y})$, the ideal $\mathfrak a$ defines an ideal lattice over $\mathcal O_F$. In this paper, we give explicit formulas for the Jordan decomposition of this lattice at a prime ideal $\mathfrak p\subset\mathcal O_F$, in terms of the prime ideal factorization of $\mathfrak a$ in $E$. Following the approach of Erez, Morales, and Perlis, we reduce the computation to the local behavior at the primes above $\mathfrak p$. Our results provide local invariants for the isometry relation between ideal lattices, with potential applications to the study of structured lattices arising in arithmetic and cryptography.

</details>


### [135] [No three algebraic conjugates of degree sixteen sum to zero](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03583)
*Žygimantas Baronėnas,Paulius Drungilas,Jonas Jankauskas*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了最小正整数 d（不为3的倍数）使存在一个次数为d的代数数其三个共轭之和为零，且 d=20。该结论源自一个更一般的定理：对于次数 d=p^m 的代数数共轭之间的整系数线性关系，系数之和必被p整除；对于 d=2p^m（p≥3）且系数绝对值和小于p的情形，系数之和为偶数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究代数数共轭之间的线性关系，特别是寻找使得三个共轭相加为零的最小可能次数，这是一个涉及数论、代数方程与线性关系的经典问题。通过限制最小的非3倍数的次数，给出精确答案 d=20。

Method: 作者首先建立了关于代数数共轭线性关系的两个关键引理：其一，当次数 d=p^m（p为素数）时，任意整系数线性关系 \sum a_j \alpha_j=0 中系数之和必被p整除；其二，当 d=2p^m（p≥3）且系数绝对值和小于p时，系数之和为偶数。利用这两个结果，通过排除法缩小候选次数，最终证明最小次数为20。

Result: 证明了最小正整数 d（不为3的倍数）使得存在次数为d的代数数其三个共轭相加为零，结果是 d=20。

Conclusion: 本文通过建立代数数共轭线性关系中系数之和的整除性质，精确刻画了三个共轭之和为零的最小代数数次数，即20，推广并深化了关于代数数共轭线性关系的研究。

Abstract: Let $d$ be the smallest positive integer, not a multiple of $3$, for which there exists an algebraic number $\al$ of degree $d$ over $\mathbb{Q}$ whose three algebraic conjugates add to zero. We prove that $d=20$. This is derived from the following result: for any linear relation $\sum_{j=1}^d a_j \al_j=0$ with coefficients $a_j\in\mathbb{Z}$ among the conjugates $\al_j$ of an algebraic number of degree $d=p^m$, where $p$ is a prime number, $m \geq 1$, the sum $\sum_{j=1}a_j$ is divisible by $p$. If $d=2p^m$, $p\geq 3$ and $\sum_{j=1}^d|a_d| < p$, then $\sum_{j=1}a_j$ is an even number.

</details>


### [136] [An upper bound for counting algebraic tori over $\mathbb{Q}$ by Artin conductor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03651)
*Jungin Lee*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了在有理数域上，n维代数环面的Artin导子以X为界时，其同构类的个数满足N_n^{tor}(X) ≪_n X^{exp(C(log n)^2)}，其中C为绝对常数。值得注意的是，证明过程是通过与ChatGPT 5.6 Pro的迭代对话开发的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究代数环面的计数问题，即对Artin导子有界的n维代数环面同构类数量给出上界估计。这是算术几何与数论中关于代数簇计数问题的一个重要方向，此前对于此类对象缺乏有效的一般性上界。

Method: 通过与ChatGPT 5.6 Pro进行迭代对话来开发主要结果的证明方法。具体技术手段涉及代数环面的算术性质与Artin导子之间的关联分析，利用群论与伽罗瓦理论工具来估计同构类的数量。

Result: 建立了一个对所有n≥2一致的上界估计：存在绝对常数C>0，使得N_n^{tor}(X)以X^{exp(C(log n)^2)}阶增长（依赖于n的隐含常数）。

Conclusion: 该结果给出了n维代数环面在Artin导子约束下的同构类计数的一个普遍上界，且该上界关于维数n的增长速度是次指数的（在指数层），具有较好的统一性。此外，作者声称证明过程借助了AI对话工具辅助完成。

Abstract: Let $N_n^{\mathrm{tor}}(X)$ be the number of isomorphism classes of $n$-dimensional algebraic tori over $\mathbb{Q}$ whose Artin conductor is bounded by $X$. We prove that there is an absolute constant $C>0$ such that, for every positive integer $n \ge 2$, $N_n^{\mathrm{tor}}(X)\ll_n X^{\exp(C(\log n)^2)}$. The proofs of the main results were developed through an iterative dialogue with ChatGPT 5.6 Pro.

</details>


### [137] [On the distribution of $φ(ψ(n))$ and $ψ(ψ(n))$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03749)
*Aimin Guo*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究Dedekind算术函数ψ(n)与欧拉函数φ(n)的复合函数φ(ψ(n))和ψ(ψ(n))的分布性质。对φ(ψ(n))给出了例外集的定量上界，改进了Sándor的密度结果；并证明了对于任意固定c>0，满足ψ(ψ(n))≤cn的整数n≤x组成的集合具有渐近密度零。方法上采用了Dixit和Bhattacharjee用于涉及Dedekind算术函数复合的筛法思想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索Dedekind函数与欧拉函数复合后的分布规律，特别是细化已知的密度结果并给出定量估计，以深化对数论中这类复合算术函数行为的理解。

Method: 采用筛法技术，借鉴Dixit和Bhattacharjee处理涉及Dedekind算术函数复合问题的思想，将其推广到φ(ψ(n))和ψ(ψ(n))的分布分析中。

Result: (1) 获得了φ(ψ(n))相关例外集的定量上界，改进了Sándor此前得到的密度结果；(2) 证明了对任意固定c>0，满足ψ(ψ(n))≤cn的整数集合在n≤x范围内具有渐近密度零。

Conclusion: 通过筛法实现了对Dedekind函数与欧拉函数复合分布更精细的刻画，既定量强化了已有的密度结论，也为该类型的复合算术函数研究提供了可推广的方法框架。

Abstract: Let $ψ(n)$ and $φ(n)$ denote Dedekind's arithmetic function and Euler's totient function, respectively. We study the distribution of the compositions $φ(ψ(n))$ and $ψ(ψ(n))$. In particular, we obtain quantitative upper bounds for the exceptional set associated with $φ(ψ(n))$, thereby refining a density result of Sándor. We also prove that, for every fixed $c>0$, the set of integers $n\leq x$ satisfying $ψ(ψ(n))\leq cn$ has asymptotic density zero. Our method adapts sieve ideas used by Dixit and Bhattacharjee to compositions involving Dedekind's arithmetic function.

</details>


### [138] [Non-split Cartan curves and a characterization of fake elliptic curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03934)
*Enric Florit*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了虚二次域上具有四元数乘法（QM）的abelian曲面。通过考虑其L-函数为平方的abelian曲面，在合理猜想下证明了A具有QM当且仅当其在至少两个素数处具有包含于非分裂Cartan群的剩余像，并对所有判别式不超过33的不定四元数判别式无条件成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于具有QM的abelian曲面的ℓ-adic Tate模分解为二维Q-有理表示的两份拷贝，目前缺乏将其与椭圆曲线所附表示区分开来的判据，因此QM abelian曲面被称为"fake elliptic curves"。本文旨在为定义在虚二次域K上的QM abelian曲面提供一个新的特征化判据。

Method: 本文基于Siksek和Michaud-Jacobs关于非分裂Cartan模曲线上二次点的工作，通过计算曲线X_ns^+(15)上的有理点，并证明X_ns(6)、X_ns(10)和X_ns(15)上的所有二次点都是非例外的（non-exceptional），从而建立QM abelian曲面的特征化判据。

Result: 在合理猜想下，证明了无潜在CM且L-函数为平方的abelian曲面A具有QM当且仅当它在至少两个素数处具有包含于非分裂Cartan群的剩余像；该特征化对所有不定四元数判别式不超过33的情形是无条件的。

Conclusion: 本文首次为虚二次域上QM abelian曲面提供了基于剩余表示像的算术判据，通过非分裂Cartan模曲线的有理点和二次点的计算实现了这一目标。

Abstract: The $\ell$-adic Tate module of an abelian surface with quaternionic multiplication (QM) decomposes as two copies of a two-dimensional $\mathbb{Q}$-rational representation. To this day, there is no criterion to distinguish these representations from those attached to elliptic curves. For this reason, QM abelian surfaces are usually called fake elliptic curves.
  In this paper we characterize QM abelian surfaces defined over an imaginary quadratic field $K$. Namely, we consider an abelian surface $A/K$ without potential CM and whose $L$-function is a square. Under a reasonable conjecture, we show that $A$ has QM if and only if it has residual image contained in a non-split Cartan group modulo at least two primes. The characterization is unconditional for all indefinite quaternion discriminants up to 33.
  The proof is based on previous work of Siksek and Michaud-Jacobs on quadratic points on non-split Cartan modular curves. In particular, we compute the rational points on the curve $X_{ns}^+(15)$, and we prove that all quadratic points on $X_{ns}(6)$, $X_{ns}(10)$ and $X_{ns}(15)$ are non-exceptional.

</details>


### [139] [On Diophantine equations over the integer rings of quadratic fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03992)
*Zhi-Wei Sun*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了任意二次数域K的代数整数环O_K上的希尔伯特第十问题，证明了不存在算法判定具有整数系数和16个未知数的多项式方程在O_K上是否有解。此外，当K为实二次域时，15个未知数足以保证不可判定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 希尔伯特第十问题在有理数域上的不可判定性已有经典结果（MRDP定理），Denef在1975年证明了其在任意二次数域代数整数环上的不可判定性，但未给出未知数个数的具体界。本文旨在确定在二次数域代数整数环上实现不可判定性所需的最小未知数个数。

Method: 作者通过构造特定的多项式方程或利用已有不可判定性结果的归约方法，对任意二次数域建立16个未知数时的不可判定性，并在实二次域情形将未知数个数进一步压缩至15个。

Result: 对于任意二次数域K，不存在算法判定整数系数且含16个未知数的多项式方程在O_K上是否有解；当K为实二次域时，该结论在15个未知数下依然成立。

Conclusion: 该结果将Denef定理在未知数个数上做了精确化，表明二次数域代数整数环上希尔伯特第十问题的不可判定性可以在非常有限的未知数个数内实现，且实二次域情形具有更优的界限。

Abstract: Let $K$ be any quadratic number field, and let $O_K$ be the ring of algebraic integers in $K$. In 1975 J. Denef proved that Hilbert's Tenth Problem over $O_K$ has a negative solution. In this paper we establish the following undecidability result: There is no algorithm to decide whether an arbitrarily given polynomial equation $P(z_1,\ldots,z_{16})=0$ (with integer coefficients and 16 unknowns) has solutions over $O_K$. Moreover, when $K$ is a real quadratic field, we show that $15$ unknowns suffice for undecidability.

</details>

### [140] [Combinatorial Bounds on the Peterson Hit Problem via Certified Matrix Minors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02623)
*Dang Vo Phuc*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文研究Peterson hit问题（模2 Steenrod代数下多项式代数的生成元最小集）。针对k≥5时精确算法不可行的问题，利用Cartan公式和Lucas定理构建稀疏矩阵模型，通过构造认证的子矩阵行列式（minors）来得到上界和下界。严格尖峰单项式完全刻画零行；零列公式给出上界；置换子行列式、三角子行列式和超图关联给出下界。特别地，同余族精确分解为F2上的单纯边界矩阵，得到闭式秩公式。这些结果表明纯组合方法的绝对极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Peterson hit问题在k≤4时已完全解决，但k≥5时基础元素的组合爆炸使精确高斯消元算法计算上不可行。因此需要绕开完全消元，开发新的组合方法以在k≥5时仍能获得可计算的界。

Method: 通过Cartan公式和Lucas定理将degree-d hit空间建模为稀疏矩阵；证明严格尖峰单项式精确刻画零行（结构上限）；推导精确零列公式并结合Sq^1层的同调和Adem关系的线性依赖来细化上界；构造独立列族（置换子行列式、无环枢轴系统的三角子行列式、超图关联的q-支撑列）以得到下界；识别出精确分解为F2上单纯边界矩阵的同余族，给出闭式秩公式。

Result: 得到了对任意k≥1且d≥0均可普遍计算的两侧界。同时建立了纯组合方法处理hit问题的绝对极限，将普适离散证书与表示论和权重滤过提供的degree-specific分辨率清楚地区分开。

Conclusion: 纯组合方法只能提供普适的离散证书来给出两侧界，无法完全解决k≥5的hit问题；更精细的degree-specific结果必须依赖表示论（Steenrod module）和权重滤过等结构工具。

Abstract: The Peterson hit problem seeks a minimal set of generators for the polynomial algebra $\mathcal P_k=\mathbb F_2[x_1,\ldots,x_k]$ as a module over the mod--2 Steenrod algebra. While completely resolved for $k \leq 4$, the unrestricted problem remains widely open for $k \geq 5$, where the combinatorial explosion of basis elements renders exact algorithmic computation intractable. To bypass full Gaussian elimination, we model the degree--$d$ hit space via a sparse matrix driven by the Cartan formula and Lucas's theorem, shifting the focus to the construction of certified matrix minors. We first prove that strict spike monomials exactly characterize the zero rows, establishing a hard structural limit on coordinate-level annihilators. To bound the matrix rank from above (cohit lower bound), we derive exact zero-column formulae, which are strictly refined by the exact homology of the $\operatorname{Sq}^1$-layer and systematic linear dependencies induced by Adem relations. To bound the rank from below (cohit upper bound), we extract explicit independent column families: singleton columns yield permutation minors, acyclic pivot systems optimize triangular minors across all row orders, and $q$-support columns are formalized through hypergraph incidence. Crucially, we identify a congruence family that decomposes precisely into simplicial boundary matrices over $\mathbb F_2$, yielding a sharp closed-form rank formula. The resulting two-sided bounds are universally computable for every $k \geq 1$ and $d \geq 0$. Significantly, these results establish the absolute limits of purely combinatorial approaches to the hit problem, cleanly separating universal discrete certificates from the degree-specific resolutions provided by representation theory and weight filtrations.

</details>


### [141] [Finite Cover Resolution Complexity of Nielsen Fixed Point Spectra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02765)
*Ahmet Selman Kaya*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文为有限连通CW复形上的自映射引入"可见Nielsen数"，通过在有限正则覆盖上优化来定义visibility profile、观测者复杂度(observer complexity)等三个分辨率阈值，并系统研究其在环面自同态、主环面丛及Heisenberg流形上的精确计算与动力学不变性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Nielsen数（Nielsen number）是估计自映射不动点个数的强有力不变量，但它依赖于全空间的信息。在实际观测中，观测者可能只能通过有限覆盖获取信息，因此经典Nielsen数中许多本质不动点类可能在有限观测下不可区分。本文旨在建立一套"由有限观测者可见"的Nielsen理论，量化不同分辨率下可区分不动点类的层级，并探索其在拓扑动力学中的新不变量（如observer complexity）的性与计算。

Method: 本文将Reidemeister迹（Reidemeister trace）推送到与自映射相容的有限正则覆盖上，从而定义可见Nielsen数。通过在所有这样的覆盖上取优化，定义visibility profile和三个分辨率阈值（包括observer complexity oc(f)）。利用profinite完备化中的扭曲共轭刻画有限观测者的不可区分性，并构造盲子群（blind subgroup）。针对环面自同态、主环面丛的相容映射以及整Heisenberg幂零流形上的标量膨胀，分别给出visibility profile的精确除数阶梯公式、最大可解kernel及oc(f)的显式表达式。还构造了具有相同经典Nielsen/Lefschetz序列、Nielsen zeta函数、微分特征值及拓扑熵但observer complexity序列指数级不同的系统。

Result: 主要结果包括：（1）有限观测者不可区分性与profinite完备化中的扭曲共轭等价的刻画；（2）构造非零Reidemeister迹但对所有有限观测者都消失的例子；（3）证明即便在诱导基本群映射、同调映射、Lefschetz数和Nielsen数全部固定时，observer complexity在同一有限复形上仍然无界；（4）环面自同态的完整visibility profile为精确的除数阶梯；（5）主环面丛在有限Reidemeister情形下唯一最大可解kernel的识别及oc(F)的公式；（6）Heisenberg流形标量膨胀的精确公式与observer complexity zeta函数；（7）构造经典不变量完全相同但observer complexity序列指数级不同的系统。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一套完整的有限观测Nielsen理论框架，表明observer complexity是一个独立于经典拓扑不动点不变量（Nielsen数、Lefschetz数、Nielsen zeta函数、拓扑熵等）的全新动力学不变量，在区分动力系统方面具有比经典不变量更强的分辨力。该工作为"有限观测下的拓扑不动点理论"开辟了新方向，并提供了环面、主环面丛和Heisenberg流形上的完整可计算结果。

Abstract: For a self-map (f\colon X\to X) of a finite connected CW complex, we introduce visible Nielsen numbers by pushing the Reidemeister trace to finite regular covers compatible with (f). Optimizing over such covers gives a visibility profile and three resolution thresholds, including the observer complexity (\operatorname{oc}(f)), the least cover degree that separates all essential Nielsen fixed-point classes. We identify finite-observer indistinguishability with twisted conjugacy in the profinite completion and determine the associated blind subgroup. We also realize nonzero Reidemeister traces that vanish for every finite observer, and show that observer complexity is unbounded on a fixed finite complex even when the induced maps on the fundamental group and homology, the Lefschetz number, and the Nielsen number are fixed. For toral endomorphisms we compute the complete visibility profile as an exact divisor staircase. For compatible maps of principal torus bundles, in the finite-Reidemeister regime, we identify the unique maximal resolving kernel and express (\operatorname{oc}(F)) in terms of the Nielsen number and the radical of the bundle characteristic class modulo the fibre map. This yields exact formulas for scalar dilations of integral Heisenberg nilmanifolds, together with observer entropy and a rational observer-complexity zeta function. Finally, we construct toral and Heisenberg systems with identical Nielsen and Lefschetz sequences, Nielsen zeta functions, differential eigenvalues, and topological entropy, but exponentially different observer-complexity sequences.

</details>


### [142] [Homotopy classification of $4$-manifolds with fundamental group $\mathbb{Z}/p \rtimes \mathbb{Z}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03245)
*Kamen Pavlov*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文研究奇素数p下，以Z/p ⋊ Z为基本群的闭4流形的同伦分类。证明了此类流形的同伦类型由二次2型（quadratic 2-type）加上一个额外的Z/p值交形式完全决定，并将Hambleton–Kreck关于Poincaré 4复形的极化同伦分类结果推广到无限群情形，同时给出一些更一般适用的部分同伦分类结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 4维流形的同伦分类是几何拓扑中的核心问题。已有结果表明，对有限基本群的闭4流形，同伦类型可由二次2型决定；但对无限基本群的情形，特别是形如Z/p ⋊ Z这样的群，此前缺乏完整的分类结果。本文旨在填补这一空白，并将已知的分类理论推广到更广泛的群类。

Method: 利用二次2型（quadratic 2-type）作为基本不变量，结合代数拓扑与手术理论（surgery theory）的工具，引入一个额外的Z/p值交形式作为补充不变量，从而给出闭4流形的完整同伦分类。同时推广Hambleton–Kreck的极化同伦分类方法到无限基本群的情形。

Result: 对于基本群为Z/p ⋊ Z（p为奇素数）的闭4流形，证明了同伦类型由二次2型及Z/p值交形式共同决定；将Hambleton–Kreck的极化同伦分类结果推广到无限群；并获得了若干更一般情形下的部分同伦分类结果。

Conclusion: 本文在4维流形同伦分类方面取得了实质性进展，特别是对一类特定的无限基本群给出了完整分类，为更一般的4维流形同伦分类问题提供了新工具和思路。

Abstract: For an odd prime $p$, we show that the homotopy type of a closed $4$-manifold with fundamental group $\mathbb{Z}/p \rtimes \mathbb{Z}$ is determined by its quadratic $2$-type together with an additional $\mathbb{Z}/p$-valued intersection form. We also give a generalization to infinite groups of a result of Hambleton--Kreck on the polarized homotopy classification of Poincaré $4$-complexes, and we prove some partial results on the homotopy classification that are applicable more generally.

</details>

# hep-ex [[Back]](#toc)

### [143] [Measurement of the Higgs boson decay to a low-mass dilepton system and a photon in $pp$ collisions at $\sqrt{s} =$ 13 and 13.6 TeV with the ATLAS detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03369)
*ATLAS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文报告了ATLAS探测器对希格斯玻色子衰变为光子和一对轻子（电子或μ子，且轻子对不变质量<30 GeV）的测量。使用2022-2024年收集的164 fb⁻¹的13.6 TeV pp碰撞数据，测得信号强度μ=0.64，观测（预期）显著性为1.7（2.5）个标准差。与2015-2018年139 fb⁻¹的13 TeV数据结果合并后，获得信号强度μ=1.03，观测（预期）显著性为3.4（3.3）个标准差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证希格斯玻色子衰变到光子和轻子对（H→ℓℓγ）这一稀有衰变道，该过程可用于探测希格斯玻色子与轻子的耦合，以及寻找超出标准模型的新物理信号。

Method: 使用ATLAS探测器在LHC采集的数据，分别利用2022-2024年13.6 TeV数据（164 fb⁻¹）和2015-2018年13 TeV数据（139 fb⁻¹）进行H→ℓℓγ搜索，并将两个数据集的测量结果进行统计组合。

Result: 单独使用新数据的信号强度为μ=0.64，观测（预期）显著性为1.7（2.5）σ；与旧数据组合后信号强度为μ=1.03，观测（预期）显著性为3.4（3.3）σ，与标准模型预期一致。

Conclusion: 组合结果在3.4 σ显著性水平上支持希格斯玻色子衰变为光子和轻子对的信号，与标准模型预测一致，但尚未达到5 σ的发现阈值，仍需要更多数据来进一步确认。

Abstract: A measurement is presented for the Higgs boson decaying into a photon and a pair of electrons or muons with an invariant mass $m_{\ell\ell} <$ 30 GeV, using $pp$ collision data at $\sqrt{s} =$ 13.6 TeV recorded by the ATLAS detector at the Large Hadron Collider during the years 2022-2024, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 164 fb$^{-1}$. The best-fit value of the signal strength parameter, defined as the ratio of the observed signal yield to the one expected in the Standard Model, is measured to be $μ= 0.64^{+0.44}_{-0.39}$ compared with the expected value of $μ= 1.00^{+0.47}_{-0.41}$. The corresponding observed (expected) signal significance is 1.7 (2.5) standard deviations under the background-only hypothesis. The result is combined with a similar search performed using 139 fb$^{-1}$ of $pp$ collision data at $\sqrt{s} =$ 13 TeV collected during the years 2015-2018. This combination gives an observed (expected) signal strength of $μ= 1.03^{+0.35}_{-0.32}\,(μ= 1.00^{+0.34}_{-0.32})$, corresponding to an observed (expected) significance of 3.4 (3.3) standard deviations.

</details>


### [144] [Evidence of the $e^+ e^-\to f_1(1285)$ reaction with the CMD-3 detector at VEPP-2000](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03417)
*R. R. Akhmetshin,A. N. Amirkhanov,A. V. Anisenkov,V. M. Aulchenko,N. S. Bashtovoy,E. V. Bedarev,D. E. Berkaev,A. E. Bondar,A. V. Bragin,D. E. Chistyakov,V. S. Denisov,E. A. Eminov,D. A. Epifanov,L. B. Epshteyn,A. L. Erofeev,G. V. Fedotovich,L. B. Fomin,A. O. Gorkovenko,A. A. Grebenuk,S. S. Gribanov,D. N. Grigoriev,F. V. Ignatov,V. L. Ivanov,A. S. Kasaev,S. V. Karpov,V. F. Kazanin,I. A. Koop,A. A. Korobov,A. N. Kozyrev,P. P. Krokovny,A. S. Kuzmin,T. A. Kuznetsov,I. B. Logashenko,P. A. Lukin,K. Yu. Mikhailov,I. V. Obraztsov,V. G. Petrochenko,N. A. Petrov,A. S. Popov,S. A. Rastigeev,Yu. A. Rogovsky,A. A. Ruban,A. E. Ryzhenenkov,A. V. Semenov,V. E. Shebalin,D. N. Shemyakin,B. A. Shwartz,D. B. Shwartz,E. P. Solodov,M. V. Timoshenko,V. M. Titov,A. A. Talyshev,S. S. Tolmachev,A. I. Vorobiov,D. S. Zhadan,Yu. V. Yudin*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文使用CMD-3探测器在VEPP-2000对撞机上搜索$e^+e^-\to f_1(1285)$反应。在质心能量1.20--1.36 GeV范围内进行扫描，并在共振峰能量1282 MeV处专门采集51.7 pb$^{-1}$数据，发现26.5±7.4个事件来自$e^+e^-\to ηπ^0π^0$反应，给出4.5σ的C偶共振产生的证据。测得产生截面为0.074±0.021±0.010 nb，对应$f_1(1285)\to e^+e^-$分支比为$(8.3±2.3±1.1)\times10^{-9}$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究$f_1(1285)$介子的轻子衰变通道，这是研究C偶数共振通过双光子相互作用在$e^+e^-$对撞中产生的罕见过程，有助于理解介子内部结构和QCD机制。

Method: 使用CMD-3探测器在VEPP-2000对撞机上采集数据：109.1 pb$^{-1}$用于质心能量1.20--1.36 GeV范围的扫描，另外在1282 MeV共振峰能量处专门采集51.7 pb$^{-1}$数据，通过$e^+e^-\to ηπ^0π^0$衰变通道寻找信号。

Result: 在共振峰能量处观察到26.5±7.4个信号事件，为4.5σ的C偶共振产生证据；测得产生截面$σ(e^+e^-\to f_1)=0.074±0.021±0.010$ nb，分支比$B(f_1(1285)\to e^+e^-)=(8.3±2.3±1.1)\times10^{-9}$。

Conclusion: 首次提供了$f_1(1285)$通过双光子相互作用在$e^+e^-$对撞反应中产生的4.5σ实验证据，并给出了该反应产生截面及到$e^+e^-$对的分支比测量值。

Abstract: A search for the $e^+ e^-\to f_1(1285)$ reaction is performed with the CMD-3 detector at the VEPP-2000 collider. Data from a scan in the center-of-mass energy range Ec.m.=1.20--1.36 GeV with 109.1 pb$^{-1}$ of the integrated luminosity is used. A dedicated 51.7 pb$^{-1}$ have been collected at the $f_1(1285)$ resonance peak energy Ec.m.=1282 MeV, where $26.5 \pm 7.4$ events have been found in the $e^+ e^-\toηπ^0π^0$ reaction. No indication of the signal is observed in the scan data. We interpret the result as 4.5$σ$ evidence of the C-even resonance production in the $e^+ e^-\to f_1(1285)\toηπ^0π^0$ reaction via the two-photon interaction. The production cross section is found to be $σ(e^+ e^-\to f_1) = 0.074 \pm 0.021 \pm 0.010$ nb, and the corresponding branching fraction to the $e^+ e^-$ pair is $B(f_1(1285)\to e^+e^-) = (8.3 \pm 2.3 \pm 1.1)\times 10^{-9}$.

</details>

Zhao,A. Zharkova,A. Zhelezov,S. Z. Zheng,X. Z. Zheng,Y. Zheng,T. Zhou,X. Zhou,Y. Zhou,V. Zhovkovska,L. Z. Zhu,X. Zhu,X. Zhu,V. Zhukov,J. Zhuo,Q. Zou,D. Zuliani,G. Zunica*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 基于LHCb实验收集的9 fb⁻¹质子-质子对撞数据，搜索了B⁺c介子衰变到粲介子与无粲介子对的过程，报告了五个分支比与碎裂比乘积的结果，其中第一个为首次观测，第二个和第三个呈现显著证据，第四个将先前测量精度提升了一倍；同时测量了B⁺c→D⁰K⁺衰变中的CP不对称性，结果与零兼容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究B⁺c介子衰变到粲介子与无粲介子对的过程，以深入理解B⁺c介子的弱衰变动力学和强子化物理，并检验CP对称性。

Method: 利用LHCb探测器采集的9 fb⁻¹质子-质子对撞数据，通过全面搜索B⁺c介子到粲介子（D⁺、D⁰、D*⁰、D⁺s）与无粲介子（K⁺、K*⁰、π⁺、φ）的五种衰变道，测量分支比与碎裂比fc/fu的乘积，并测量D⁰K⁺道的CP不对称性。

Result: 五个分支比乘积结果中：B⁺c→D⁺K*⁰为首次观测（1.42×10⁻⁶）；B⁺c→D*⁰K⁺和B⁺c→D⁺sφ呈现显著证据；B⁺c→D⁰K⁺将此前测量精度提升两倍；B⁺c→D⁰π⁺给出95%置信度上限。CP不对称性测量结果与零兼容。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了B⁺c介子衰变到粲-无粲介子对的新测量结果，包括首次观测、显著证据和精度提升，为B⁺c弱衰变机制提供了更多实验约束。

Abstract: Using proton-proton collision data corresponding to an integrated luminosity of $9 \,\textrm{fb}^{-1}$ collected by the LHCb experiment, searches are performed for $B^+_c$ mesons decaying to a charm and a charmless meson pair. Five products of branching fraction, ${\cal B}(B^+_c\!\to DX)$, and fragmentation ratio $f_c\big/f_u$ are reported, \begin{align*} R_{D^+ K^{*0}} &= ( 1.42 \pm 0.23 \pm 0.07 \pm 0.11 ) \times 10^{-6}, \\ R_{D^{*0} K^+} &= (1.46 \pm 0.30 \pm 0.11 \pm 0.05 ) \times 10^{-6}, \\ R_{D^+_s φ}\ \ \ &= ( 4.0\pm 1.3 \pm 0.2 \pm 0.5) \times 10^{-7 }, \\ R_{D^0 K^+}\ &= ( 9.7 \pm 1.0 \pm 0.4 \pm 0.3 ) \times 10^{-7}, \\ R_{D^0 π^+}\ &<\ 1.4 \times 10^{-7}\ \text{at 95\% CL}. \end{align*} In each case, the first uncertainty is statistical, the second is systematic and the third includes external uncertainties. The first result is a first observation, the second and third exhibit clear evidence and the fourth improves the precision of previous measurements by a factor two. Additionally, the $CP$ asymmetry in $B^+_c\!\to D^0 K^+$ decays is measured and found to be compatible with zero.

</details>


### [146] [Timing-Based Search for Magnetic Monopoles with the NOvA Detector on the Surface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03888)
*NOvA Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: NOvA实验远探测器在2743天有效曝光中搜索宇宙线磁单极子成分，未观测到符合事件，在90%置信水平下对速度范围6×10⁻⁴<β<5×10⁻³、质量大于10⁹ GeV的磁单极子设定了8×10⁻¹⁶ cm⁻²s⁻¹sr⁻¹的通量上限。由于NOvA仅3米水当量的浅层覆盖，该约束覆盖了此前未被探索的低质量区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁单极子是粒子物理学中长期追寻但尚未发现的理论粒子，实验中通过搜索宇宙线中的磁单极子成分可检验相关理论预言。NOvA实验浅层覆盖的特点使其能够触及此前实验（多为深层地下探测器）难以覆盖的低质量磁单极子区域。

Method: 利用NOvA实验的14千吨分段液体闪烁体远探测器，在2743活天的曝光时间内搜索宇宙线磁单极子成分。该探测器专为观测GeV尺度电子中微子而设计，浅层覆盖（3米水当量）使低速磁单极子能够到达探测器。

Result: 未观测到与磁单极子一致的事件，在90%置信水平下对速度6×10⁻⁴<β<5×10⁻³、质量>10⁹ GeV的磁单极子通量设定上限为8×10⁻¹⁶ cm⁻²s⁻¹sr⁻¹。

Conclusion: NOvA实验利用其浅层覆盖优势，首次覆盖了此前未被探索的低质量磁单极子区域，尽管未发现信号，但给出了该区域迄今最强的通量上限约束。

Abstract: We report a search for a magnetic monopole component of the cosmic-ray flux in a 2743-live-day exposure of the NOvA experiment's Far Detector, a 14 kt segmented liquid scintillator detector designed primarily to observe GeV-scale electron neutrinos. No events consistent with monopoles were observed, setting an upper limit on the flux of $8\times 10^{-16}~\mathrm{cm^{-2}s^{-1}sr^{-1}}$ at 90% C.L. for monopole speed $6\times 10^{-4} < β< 5\times 10^{-3}$ and mass greater than $10^{9}$ GeV. Because of NOvA's small overburden of 3 meters-water equivalent, this constraint covers a previously unexplored low-mass region.

</details>
